We propose Raptor, a training-free framework that uses rejection feedback to jointly regulate candidate coverage and drafting effort. Our key insight is that acceptance length alone cannot distinguish insufficient candidate coverage from excessive deep expansion, and these conditions require different allocation decisions. Raptor therefore extracts complementary rejection-aware signals to characterize speculative progress, frontier coverage, and redundant expansion. These signals jointly guide bounded, momentum-smoothed updates of tree depth, branching width, and drafting budget, together with execution control that limits unnecessary drafting and progressively restores expansion when appropriate. Extensive experiments with two VLMs across seven multimodal benchmarks demonstrate the effectiveness of Raptor, achieving up to 2.39x average speedup over autoregressive decoding.

</details>


### [36] [Wearable Logo, Silent Hijack: Stealthy Cross-Modal Semantic Attacks on Visible--Infrared Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25437)
*Qike Zhang,Jiaju Han,Xuelian Shi,Xiang Chen,Dehe Zhao,Banglong Nong,Wenhao Zheng,Lei Wang,Jiahuan Long,Jiujiang Guo,Chengyin Hu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出LoSA：在保持商标轮廓不变的前提下优化其内部外观与放置位置，实现对可见光-红外视觉语言模型的协同定向语义攻击。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 商标在服饰与日用品上普遍存在，其可辨识形状与语义联想使其自然融入真实环境且视觉上不显眼，因而可能成为跨模态定向语义攻击的隐蔽载体；现有方法难以在固定形状内实现高表达力扰动并兼顾VIS/IR外观差异与模型响应差异。

Method: 基于分数的黑盒框架：用轮廓场与低频图谱模态构建内部颜色布局，用冻结的外观适配器耦合可见光与红外外观，再用粒子群优化联合优化共享设计参数（内部外观与放置位置），全程保持轮廓不变。

Result: 在四个数据集上，LoSA在同时（VIS-IR）Top-1定向成功率上稳定优于所评估的基线；跨架构实验、视觉自然度评分以及描述生成/VQA迁移分析进一步刻画了其有效性与局限。

Conclusion: 真实环境中的常见视觉符号可作为保形载体，实现跨成像模态的协同定向语义攻击，揭示了VLM在商标类语义线索上的安全风险。

Abstract: Logos appear naturally on clothing and daily necessities, where their recognizable shapes and semantic associations make them familiar visual cues. This familiarity helps them blend into real-world environments and remain visually inconspicuous. We ask whether an existing logo can serve as a carrier for coordinated targeted semantic attacks on visible-infrared (VIS-IR) vision-language models (VLMs) without changing its original outline. This setting requires expressive adversarial variation within a fixed shape while accounting for different appearance characteristics and model responses across modalities. We propose Logo-guided Semantic Attack (LoSA), a score-based black-box framework that optimizes a logo's interior appearance and placement while preserving its silhouette. LoSA uses a contour field and low-frequency graph spectral modes to structure the interior color layout, a frozen appearance adapter to couple VIS and IR appearances, and particle swarm optimization to jointly optimize shared design parameters. Experiments across four datasets demonstrate that LoSA consistently outperforms the evaluated baselines in simultaneous Top-1 targeted success. Cross-architecture experiments, visual naturalness ratings, and captioning/VQA transfer analyses further characterize its effectiveness and limitations. These findings reveal that familiar visual symbols from real-world environments can serve as shape-preserving carriers for coordinated targeted semantic attacks across imaging modalities.

</details>


### [37] [VisualTalk-Bench: Evaluating Question-Driven Avatar-Led Video Explanations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25444)
*Xin Zhang,Tujie Xu,Zhiyi Chen,Xiaodong Zhang,Sicheng Yang,Minglei Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文提出 VisualTalk-Bench，用于评估"问题驱动的数字人主导视频讲解"任务，覆盖金融与中英教育场景，含 800 条参考视频与 100 条独立评测请求，从内容事实性、语音-视觉一致性、视觉质量、动作平滑度和"数字人对内容的指向性"五个维度评测；并给出模块化参考实现 Avatar-O Agent。实验表明事实精度与视觉质量不能保证指向准确，Avatar-O Agent 在 26 人用户研究中取得最高评分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视频生成与数字人合成的进步使得通过视觉解释回答用户问题成为可能，但现有基准很少评估数字人是否真正指向其正在讲解的视觉内容，缺乏对该任务系统性评测的手段。

Method: 构建 VisualTalk-Bench 基准（800 条精选参考视频 + 100 条独立评测请求），定义五项评测维度（其中 Avatar-Content Pointing 为核心新增维度）；并提出 Avatar-O Agent——一个具备显式交互规划的模块化参考实现，对六种智能体进行对比实验，另开展 26 名参与者的用户研究比较四种智能体。

Result: 六种智能体实验显示，事实精度与视觉质量并不能保证准确的指向行为；26 人用户研究中，Avatar-O Agent 在视觉引导、感知指向准确性和讲解有效性三项上获得最高平均评分。

Conclusion: 现有端到端视频生成器难以将手势与具体视觉目标对齐，"指向性"应作为独立且关键的评测维度；模块化的显式交互规划是提升数字人视觉讲解效果的有效路径，数据与代码将开源。

Abstract: Advances in video generation and avatar synthesis enable new ways to answer user questions through visual explanations. This task requires a system to formulate a correct answer, translate relevant information into visual material, and deliver the explanation through an avatar's speech and actions. Existing benchmarks rarely assess whether avatars point to the visual content being discussed. We introduce VisualTalk-Bench for question-driven avatar-led video explanations in finance and Chinese and English education. It includes 800 curated reference videos and 100 independent evaluation requests, assessing Content Factuality, Speech-Visual Consistency, Visual Quality, Motion Smoothness, and Avatar-Content Pointing. Yet end-to-end video generators often struggle to align gestures with specific visual targets. To explore this challenge, we develop Avatar-O Agent, a modular reference implementation with explicit interaction planning. Experiments across six agents show that factual precision and visual quality do not guarantee accurate pointing. A 26-participant study comparing four agents finds that Avatar-O Agent receives the highest average ratings for visual guidance, perceived pointing accuracy, and explanation effectiveness. We will release the data and code.

</details>


### [38] [Beyond Resemblance: Image Fusion Reimagined](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25453)
*Hao Zhang,Yumin Huang,Linfeng Tang,Junjun Jiang,Jiayi Ma*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出生成式图像融合新范式ApexFuse，以"学习高质量场景表示的通用分布"替代"源图像相似性约束"，借助人类-LMM闭环构建目标分布参考并迁移FLUX生成先验，实现多模态融合并具备跨任务泛化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 图像融合缺乏ground-truth融合图像，现有深度方法只能通过"融合结果同时近似多张源图像"间接定义目标；但不同模态成像特性差异导致相互竞争的约束，抑制互补信息的有效整合。

Method: （1）构建human-LMM-in-the-loop流水线，利用大型多模态生成模型与迭代人类反馈将抽象融合目标转化为可靠视觉参考，定义理想融合结果的目标分布；（2）通过目标分布引导的适配，将预训练FLUX的生成先验迁移至图像融合，实现以多模态观测为条件的高质量场景重建。

Result: 实验显示ApexFuse协调整合显著热目标、自然色彩与精细纹理，视觉质量优于源相似性方法，语义分割与目标检测性能亦更优；并在无需额外微调下从红外-可见光融合泛化到多曝光、多聚焦、可见光-深度融合。

Conclusion: 以分布学习替代源相似性约束是可行且有效的融合范式，生成先验+人类反馈定义的参考分布可突破传统融合目标定义瓶颈，并展现强跨任务泛化能力。

Abstract: Image fusion often lacks ground-truth fused images, forcing existing deep methods to indirectly define fusion objectives by requiring the fused image to simultaneously resemble multiple source images. However, the distinct imaging characteristics of different modalities can impose competing constraints on the fused result, potentially limiting the effective integration of their complementary information. To address this long-standing challenge facing the image fusion community, we propose ApexFuse, a novel generative image fusion paradigm that moves beyond source-resemblance constraints toward learning a general distribution of high-quality scene representations. Specifically, we develop a human-LMM-in-the-loop pipeline that leverages large multimodal generative models and iterative human feedback to translate abstract fusion objectives into reliable visual references, thereby defining the target distribution of ideal fusion outcomes. Through target-distribution-guided adaptation, we transfer the rich generative priors of pretrained FLUX to image fusion, enabling high-quality scene reconstruction conditioned on multimodal observations. Extensive experiments demonstrate that ApexFuse harmoniously integrates salient thermal targets, natural colors, and fine textures, achieving superior visual quality as well as semantic segmentation and object detection performance over previous source-resemblance methods. Moreover, ApexFuse exhibits remarkable cross-task generalization, extending from infrared-visible fusion to multi-exposure fusion, multi-focus fusion, and visible-depth fusion without additional fine-tuning.

</details>


### [39] [Lossless Latent Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25454)
*Tongda Xu,Yu Zhang,Jun Hu,Huijuan Huang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出无损隐扩散模型LLDM：不修改扩散模型本身，而是构造无损变换将像素映射到更适合扩散建模的表示，兼顾像素级精确重建与生成质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 既有实现像素精确重建的方法通常直接在像素空间建模，需改动扩散训练目标或模型架构；作者希望绕开对扩散模型本身的修改，转而优化输入表示。

Method: 构造可逆（无损）变换，用残差隐变换（residual latent transformation）与非均匀隐缩放（uneven latent scaling）将像素映射到隐表示，再在该表示上训练扩散模型；并提出语义秩优先级（SRP）指标解释其有效性。

Result: LLDM在生成质量上显著优于以往像素空间扩散模型，甚至超过若干常用有损VAE；SRP与下游生成性能呈强相关。

Conclusion: 通过无损表示变换而非修改扩散模型，可在保持像素级精确重建的同时提升生成质量；SRP可作为表征可扩散性的有效度量。

Abstract: We study the problem of training image diffusion models while preserving pixel-exact reconstruction. Prior works typically address this problem by directly modeling images in pixel space, requiring modifications to the diffusion training objective or model architecture. In this work, we introduce a different paradigm: rather than modifying diffusion models, we construct a lossless transformation that maps pixels into a representation more amenable to diffusion modeling. We term our approach the Lossless Latent Diffusion Model (LLDM). We construct LLDM using residual latent transformation and uneven latent scaling, and explain its effectiveness through a proposed metric, semantic rank priority (SRP). Empirically, LLDM substantially outperforms prior pixel-space diffusion models in generation quality and even surpasses several widely used lossy VAEs. Furthermore, we show that SRP is strongly correlated with downstream generative performance.

</details>


### [40] [CANDOR: Contrastive Self-Denoising Rewards for Label-Free Text-to-Image Post-Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25457)
*Junhyeok Lee,Byung-Hoon Kim,Kyu Sung Choi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出内在奖励 CANDOR：用同一噪声探针下"无条件/条件去噪误差的对数比"衡量提示词对去噪的贡献，可替代需偏好标注的外部奖励，作为 Flow-GRPO 唯一奖励提升组合生成与文字渲染。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 T2I 后训练 RL 依赖外部奖励模型，需标注偏好或辅助评估器，且易被 reward hacking。已有的基于生成器自身去噪精度的内在奖励（绝对自置信度）对提示忠实度是较差的代理：同提示组内该指标被图像复杂度主导，区分正确/错误样本接近随机。

Method: CANDOR（Contrastive Alignment from Null-referenced Denoising lOg-Ratio）：在共享噪声探针下，计算无条件去噪误差与条件去噪误差的对数比值，度量"提示词帮助模型去噪其自身样本的程度"。由于比值可抵消对提示无关、同时缩放两种误差的难度因素，得到提示相关性的相对度量。

Result: 同提示两样本配对比较中，CANDOR 分别在 60%（组合生成）与 63%（文字渲染）的样本对中把更优者排在前面。作为 flow-matching 模型 Flow-GRPO 的唯一奖励，CANDOR 在文字渲染、美学与人类偏好分数上优于基模型和先前的绝对自置信度奖励，且文字渲染与偏好增益无法由更强的 classifier-free guidance 复现；在已用外部奖励训练的公开 Flow-GRPO 适配器上进一步提升 PickScore 与 ImageReward，可与外部奖励互补。

Conclusion: 以内置、无需外部标注的相对自置信度作为奖励是可行方向；CANDOR 兼具独立可用性与对外部奖励的互补性，代码已开源。

Abstract: Post-training text-to-image generators with reinforcement learning usually relies on external reward models that need annotated preferences or auxiliary evaluators and are prone to reward hacking. We show that the generator's own denoising accuracy, used by a recent intrinsic reward, is a poor proxy for prompt fidelity. Within a same-prompt group, this absolute self-confidence is dominated by image complexity and ranks correct samples above incorrect ones near chance. We introduce CANDOR (Contrastive Alignment from Null-referenced Denoising lOg-Ratio), the log-ratio of unconditional to conditional denoising errors under shared noise probes, which measures how much the prompt helps the model denoise its own sample. It cancels prompt-independent difficulty that scales both errors and ranks the better of two same-prompt samples higher in 60% and 63% of pairs on compositional generation and text rendering. As the only Flow-GRPO reward for a flow-matching model, CANDOR improves text rendering, aesthetic and human-preference scores over the base model and a prior absolute self-confidence reward, with text-rendering and preference gains that stronger classifier-free guidance does not reproduce. On public Flow-GRPO adapters trained on external rewards, CANDOR further raises PickScore and ImageReward, so it can complement those rewards. Code is available at https://github.com/jhlee0619/candor.

</details>


### [41] [RemoteVLPG: How to Obtain a Specific VLM for Language-Guided Path Generation on Remote Sensing Images?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25458)
*Junzhe Dang,Mengmeng Zhang,Yuxiang Zhang,Wei Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文研究面向遥感图像的、由语言引导的起终点路径生成任务，如何获得任务专用的视觉语言模型。作者从架构比较、预训练数据构建和评测基准三方面展开，并验证了所构建预训练数据能带来优于部分商用VLM的零样本性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 面向遥感图像的language-guided path generation缺少任务专用的VLM，现有通用（含商用）VLM在该任务上效果有限，且缺乏系统的架构对比、可扩展的预训练数据与规范化评测手段。

Method: 1）实现并对比三种常用的VLM架构方案；2）设计可扩展的预训练数据构建方法，构建具备一定多样性的有效预训练数据集；3）构建三个支持分层（stratified）评测的基准，并设计三项针对路径生成的评价指标；配合大量实验进行分析。

Result: 在所构建预训练数据集上训练的方法展现出较好的零样本性能，其零样本表现可超过部分先进商用VLM；并给出三种架构的性能对比结论。

Conclusion: 通过架构对比、可扩展预训练数据构建与规范化基准/evaluation metrics设计，可有效获得遥感语言引导路径生成的任务专用VLM；代码与数据集已开源。

Abstract: We study how to obtain a task-specific Vision-Language Model (VLM) for language-guided path generation between start and end points on remote sensing images. We explore this question from three aspects. First, we implement three widely used architectural approaches and compare their performance. Second, we design a scalable method for constructing pre-training data and build an effective pre-training dataset with a degree of diversity. Methods trained on this dataset achieve promising zero-shot performance. Third, we construct three benchmarks that support stratified evaluation and design three metrics for path generation. We investigate this problem through extensive experiments. The results show that the zero-shot performance of methods trained on the constructed pre-training dataset can exceed that of some advanced commercial VLMs. Code and Dataset can be available at https://github.com/jzdang/RemoteVLPG

</details>


### [42] [ActMem: From Latent Memory Reasoning to Grounded Robotic Action](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25461)
*Linfeng Dong,Jianwei Niu,Jiuyi Zhao,Ruibing Feng,Xinghao Wu,Wu Yang,Shuai Tao,Wulong Liu,Mu Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ActMem 提出一种上下文记忆框架，在单一视觉-语言骨干内将记忆推理与感知接地解耦：用时间戳交互历史推断当前子任务并保留隐状态作为 Latent Memory Chain-of-Thought，条件化流匹配动作专家，并通过 Perception Attention 让专家直接关注当前视图，配合可无损复用计算的 Efficient Memory CoT Inference。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有记忆增强 VLA 存在两类缺陷：一类仅检索过去观测而不推理其含义，另一类在共享上下文中对完整历史进行推理，从而挤占当前感知。认知科学将二者视为不同系统——记忆重构过去经验以决定"做什么"，感知引导"怎么做"。

Method: 以具备原生视频理解能力的视觉-语言模型为骨干；从带时间戳的交互历史推断当前子任务，并将该推理过程产生的隐藏状态保留为 Latent Memory CoT，作为流匹配动作专家的条件；Perception Attention 使动作专家可直接关注当前视图；Efficient Memory CoT Inference 在连续决策间复用计算以加速长历史推理且不改变输出。

Result: RoboMemArena 上任务成功率 71.1%、完成成功率 80.8%，较最强基线分别提升 7.5 与 8.7 个百分点；MIKASA-Robo 上平均成功率 73.8%，为所有对比方法中最佳；真实世界中在外生、内生与无痕记忆任务上平均成功率 68.8%，高于最强记忆增强 VLA 10.0 个百分点。

Conclusion: 将记忆推理与感知接地解耦的上下文记忆设计，可在仿真与真实长程操作任务上稳定超越现有记忆增强 VLA，同时通过计算复用维持长历史下的推理效率。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models have shown promising results in real-world long-horizon manipulation, where memory plays a crucial role.
  However, existing memory-augmented VLAs either retrieve past observations without reasoning about what they imply, or reason over the full
  history in a shared context where it crowds out current perception. Cognitive science treats them as distinct systems: memory reconstructs past
  experience to decide what to do, while perception guides how to act. We therefore propose ActMem, an in-context memory framework that
  decouples memory reasoning from perceptual grounding within a single vision-language backbone. Built on a vision-language model with native
  video understanding, ActMem infers the current subtask from a timestamped interaction history and keeps the hidden states formed during this
  inference as a Latent Memory Chain-of-Thought (Memory CoT), which conditions a flow-matching action expert. Perception Attention further lets
  the expert attend directly to the current view. To keep inference fast under long histories, Efficient Memory CoT Inference reuses computation
  across consecutive decisions without changing the output. ActMem achieves 71.1% task success and 80.8% completion success on RoboMemArena,
  surpassing the strongest baseline by 7.5 and 8.7 percentage points, and 73.8% average success on MIKASA-Robo, the best among all compared
  methods. In the real world, it reaches 68.8% average success on exogenous, endogenous, and traceless memory tasks, 10.0 percentage points above
  the strongest memory-augmented VLA. Code and checkpoints will be released at https://github.com/LigphiDonk/ActMem and
  https://huggingface.co/donk111/ActMem.

</details>


### [43] [A Wolf in Sheep's Pixels: Visual Camouflage Attacks via HDR Gain Maps](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25469)
*Qi Shen,Yujia Gong,Yue Zhao,Tong Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: DRJanus 利用 SDR 处理与 HDR 显示的重建差异，在单张 JPEG 中嵌入两幅独立图像，实现视觉伪装攻击。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 图像可在通过自动化安全检测的同时向人类展示有害内容，根源在于标准动态范围（SDR）处理与高动态范围（HDR）显示之间存在信任鸿沟：自动系统只读 SDR 基图，而 HDR 显示器依据增益图重建另一外观。

Method: 提出 DRJanus 攻击：对 HDR 重建过程求逆，将两幅独立选定的彩色图像嵌入同一 JPEG；精确保留 SDR 基图，并通过辅助增益图在 HDR 端重建目标图像，无需查询或针对目标模型进行优化。

Result: 400 张图像上 HDR 目标重建平均 PSNR 43.03 dB；在 100 张性内容基准上，绕过全部 6 家商用审核服务与 9 个视觉语言模型；5 个智能体各 120 例任务中，审核规避成功率 31.67%–100%，任务操纵成功率 95%–100%。

Conclusion: 揭示了视觉信任缺口：保障机器所读取的内容并不等于保障用户所看到的内容，并初步探讨跨视图一致性检查作为缓解手段。

Abstract: An image can pass automated safety checks while showing harmful content to its human viewer. We introduce DRJanus, a visual camouflage attack that exploits the gap between standard dynamic range (SDR) processing and high dynamic range (HDR) display. Automated systems often read only an image's SDR base, whereas HDR viewers reconstruct its appearance using an auxiliary gain map. DRJanus inverts this reconstruction to embed two independently chosen color images in a single JPEG, without querying or optimizing against the target model. It preserves the chosen SDR base exactly and reconstructs HDR targets at an average PSNR of 43.03 dB across 400 images. On a benchmark of 100 sexual-content images, DRJanus evades detection by all six tested commercial moderation services and nine vision-language models. Across five agents, each evaluated on 120 task cases per threat setting, attack success ranges from 31.67% to 100% for moderation evasion and from 95% to 100% for task manipulation. We also explore cross-view consistency checks as a mitigation. These findings expose a gap in visual trust: securing what a machine reads does not secure what a user sees. A demo is available at https://qshen0629.github.io/DRJanus/

</details>


### [44] [TuringPAI: A Foundation for On-Device Physical AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25474)
*Qiman Wu,Hanlin Chen,Yuhua Wei,Mingyuan Wang,Shaman Tang,Minghao Li,Rui Xin,Xingye Liu,Zhichao Yang,Yiqi Yu,Hongyan Pei,Xiaowen Tian,YingYing Liu,Shiwen Liu,Jiahui Hu,Yizhao Wang,Yuheng Zhang,Anhua Liu,Yue He,Shuang Su,Da Zhu,Hua Zhou,Pengfei Diao,Haojie Yang,Weichao Huang,Hang Zhang,Honggou Yang,Xianming Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 TuringPAI 系列端侧 Physical AI 模型：0.65B 视觉语言基础模型 TuringPAI-VL（HybridViT-MLQ 架构），及其下游 TuringPAI-Drive（自动驾驶）与 TuringPAI-Embodied（机器人操作）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Physical AI 需要在有限车载/机载算力下理解空间关系与动态环境，并将观测转化为动作；现有方案缺乏可共享、紧凑且具备几何感知能力的视觉语言基座。

Method: 构建 0.65B 的 TuringPAI-VL：HybridViT-MLQ 将卷积局部聚合与 Transformer 全局建模结合；Multi-scale Latent Queries 在同一视觉 Transformer 内融合多尺度空间特征与观测历史，形成紧凑结构化接口供语言模型使用；训练混合通用视觉语言数据与几何、跨视角、时序监督。该模型作为 Drive 与 Embodied 两个 VLA 模型的共享初始化。

Result: TuringPAI-VL 在定量几何感知上优于 Qwen2-VL-2B；TuringPAI-Drive 采用 MLQ 接口时各项 ADE/FDE 指标与全 token HybridViT 基线差距在 1.2% 以内，内部仿真评测聚合得分可比，并在单颗 NVIDIA Orin-X 上通过 XPeng Native Runtime（XNRT）实现 12 Hz 车载轨迹生成；TuringPAI-Embodied 在四个 LIBERO 套件上平均成功率 96.8%。

Conclusion: 结果验证了在实用端侧约束下，可共享的视觉语言基座能够同时支撑自动驾驶与机器人操作，MLQ 紧凑接口在几乎不损失性能的前提下实现高效部署。

Abstract: Physical AI requires models that understand spatial relationships and dynamic environments and translate observations into actions under limited onboard compute. We present TuringPAI, a family of models for on-device Physical AI comprising TuringPAI-VL for shared vision-language understanding, TuringPAI-Drive for autonomous driving, and TuringPAI-Embodied for robotic manipulation. TuringPAI-VL is a 0.65B model built on HybridViT-MLQ, which combines convolutional local aggregation with Transformer-based global modeling. Multi-scale Latent Queries integrate spatial features and observation history within the same visual Transformer, providing a compact, structured interface to the language model. Training combines general vision-language data with geometric, cross-view, and temporal supervision. TuringPAI-VL supports general multimodal understanding and improves quantitative geometric perception over Qwen2-VL-2B, while providing a shared initialization for the two downstream VLA models. For autonomous driving, TuringPAI-Drive maintains all evaluated ADE/FDE metrics within 1.2\% of a full-token HybridViT baseline using the MLQ interface and achieves comparable aggregate scores in an in-house simulation evaluation. Using XPeng Native Runtime (XNRT) on a single NVIDIA Orin-X SoC, TuringPAI-Drive generates trajectories at 12\,Hz in on-vehicle operation. For robotic manipulation, TuringPAI-Embodied achieves a 96.8\% average success rate across the four LIBERO suites. These results demonstrate a shared vision-language foundation for driving and robotic manipulation under practical on-device constraints.

</details>


### [45] [SegAnyFruit: Learning Generalizable and Edge Deployable Fruit Instance Segmentation at Scale](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25475)
*Yanan Wang,Yuzheng wang,Yibin Ying,Zhenghao Fei*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出SegAnyFruit (SAF)，一个数据构建与渐进式训练框架，将基础模型先验蒸馏到轻量、免提示的水果实例分割器中，实现跨物种、跨果园、跨成像条件的零样本泛化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在真实农业场景部署水果实例分割，需在严格的边缘端算力与标注预算下，泛化到未见过的物种、果园与成像条件，现有方法难以兼顾泛化性与部署效率。

Method: 三段式渐进训练：1) 通用掩码预训练；2) 基于自建WebFruits数据集中自动标注的网络图像进行广泛中训练；3) 基于PaperFruits数据集进行人工核验的农业精调后训练。整体覆盖165种水果、733K图像、9.66M整果掩码，最终蒸馏为免提示分割器（SAF-RF-DETR）。

Result: 在7个基准上，零样本SAF-RF-DETR-XL平均掩码AP50:95达69.06（较SAM 3提升+4.76），且可部署于边缘设备；SAF初始化在5个农业域上提升少样本到全样本适配并缓解灾难性遗忘；系统分析训练阶段、数据规模、模型容量与生成式增强；并验证流程可迁移至水下、细胞与人体分割。

Conclusion: 通过基础模型先验蒸馏加多阶段数据与训练设计，可在低标注与低算力约束下获得强泛化、可边缘部署的水果分割器，且该框架具备跨域可迁移性；作者将开源数据集、代码与模型。

Abstract: Deploying fruit instance segmentation in real agricultural settings requires generalizing across unseen species, orchards, and imaging conditions under strict onboard compute and annotation budgets. We introduce SegAnyFruit (SAF), a data construction and progressive training framework that distills foundation-model priors into lightweight, prompt-free segmenters. SAF combines generic mask pre-training, broad mid-training on automatically annotated web imagery from our curated WebFruits dataset, and human-verified agricultural refinement post-training on PaperFruits, together spanning 733K images and 9.66M whole-fruit masks across 165 fruit types. Across seven benchmarks, zero-shot SAF-RF-DETR-XL reaches 69.06 average mask AP50:95 (+4.76 over SAM 3) while remaining deployable on edge devices. SAF initialization also boosts few-shot to full-shot adaptation across five agricultural domains while mitigating catastrophic forgetting. Finally, we systematically analyze training stages, data scale, model capacity, and generative augmentation, and validate our pipeline's transferability to underwater, cell, and human segmentation. We will release our datasets, code, and SAF models.

</details>


### [46] [TAP: Tapping the Discriminative Potential of Frozen Representations for Class-Incremental Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25478)
*Zihao Wang,Yu-Wei Zhan,Xiao-Qian Liu,Zhen-Duo Chen,Weitong Liu,Xin Luo,Xin-Shun Xu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出TAP框架，在冻结的CLIP嵌入空间中仅用轻量优化累积类信息，实现类增量学习（CIL）的SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预训练模型虽能提供可迁移表示，但顺序适应会带来优化开销并破坏旧类特征结构；因此探索在保持编码器冻结的前提下，持续累积类信息以进一步挖掘表示的判别潜力。

Method: TAP包含三个模块：(1) 类条件视觉分布建模，保留类均值与类内散度，结合类特定与共享协方差稳定分布估计；(2) 类自适应语义校准，基于当前任务图像学习文本模板的类特定权重，使文本表示适配下游类；(3) 视觉-语义后验融合，通过加权专家乘积对所有已见类进行预测，且不保留旧任务图像。

Result: 在两种增量协议下的九个数据集上取得SOTA，平均增量准确率与最终阶段准确率均为对比方法中最高。

Conclusion: 冻结编码器并仅在嵌入空间累积类信息，是一种高效且有效的CIL方案，可充分挖掘预训练表示的判别能力而不引发灾难性遗忘。

Abstract: Pre-trained models provide transferable representations for class-incremental learning (CIL). However, sequentially adapting these representations introduces optimization costs and may disrupt old-class feature structure. This motivates us to explore whether the discriminative potential of these representations can be further exploited by continually accumulating class information while keeping the encoders frozen. To this end, we propose TAP, a new and novel framework for tapping the discriminative potential of frozen representations in class-incremental learning. It accumulates visual and semantic class information in the frozen CLIP embedding space with only lightweight optimization. TAP comprises three modules: class-conditional visual distribution modeling, class-adaptive semantic calibration, and visual-semantic posterior fusion. The first module retains class means and within-class scatters, combining class-specific and shared covariances to stabilize distribution estimation. The second module learns class-specific weights over text templates from current-task images, adapting text representations to downstream classes. The third module combines the visual and semantic posteriors through a weighted product of experts for prediction over all seen classes without retaining images from previous tasks. Across nine datasets under two incremental protocols, TAP achieves state-of-the-art performance, attaining the highest average incremental and final-stage accuracies among the compared methods. The code is provided as an ancillary file.

</details>


### [47] [PRIO: Pre-trained Representation for Interactive Objects in 4D Worlds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25500)
*Shixun Huang,Lifu Wei,Yue Chang,Chen Geng,Heng Zhang,Jinhyung Ahn,Caoliwen Wang,Siyuan Chen,Donglai Xiang,Jiajun Wu,Peter Yichen Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: PRIO 是一个预训练的交互式物体表示：给定静态几何，单次前向传播预测物体专属的构型空间，为拉格朗日力学提供广义坐标，使物理规律与用户交互驱动的动力学可在交互帧率下运行而无需再次网络推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 3D 世界虽可大规模采集、重建与生成，但其中物体保持静止（枕头不会被压陷、门不会被推转），缺乏描述物体如何运动、变形并实时响应物理与用户操作的表示；4D 生成方法在速度与对用户动作的遵循度上亦有不足。

Method: 提出 PRIO 预训练表示：以静态几何为输入，一次前向预测物体专属构型空间，作为拉格朗日力学的广义坐标；构型在该坐标下线性，故动力学所需量可预计算，从而以交互速率求解动力学而无需额外网络推理；在多样 4D 轨迹上预训练以获得可泛化到未见形状的构型先验；支持网格、点云与 3D 高斯表示。

Result: 在真实到仿真（real-to-sim）工作流与机器人操作中验证其可用性；与现有 4D 生成方法相比，PRIO 生成动力学更快，且更忠实地遵循用户动作。

Conclusion: PRIO 通过"预测构型空间 + 广义坐标下的预计算力学"范式，为重建与生成场景中的静态物体赋予实时、可控的交互式 4D 动力学，实现无需逐帧网络推理的交互式世界模型。

Abstract: 3D worlds can now be captured, reconstructed, and generated at scale, yet the objects in them stay still: a pressed pillow does not compress, and a pulled door does not swing. Bringing these objects into interactive 4D world models requires a representation of how they can move, deform, and respond physically to user actions in real time. We present PRIO, a pre-trained representation for interactive objects. Given static geometry, PRIO predicts an object-specific configuration space in a single forward pass. This space provides generalized coordinates for Lagrangian mechanics, allowing physical laws and user actions to govern motion. Configurations are linear in these coordinates, allowing the quantities needed by the dynamics to be precomputed. Dynamics then run at interactive rates without further network inference. Pre-trained on diverse 4D trajectories, PRIO learns priors over object configurations that generalize to unseen shapes. It supports meshes, point clouds, and 3D Gaussians, bringing objects in reconstructed and generated scenes into interaction. We demonstrate its use in real-to-sim workflows and robot manipulation. In addition, compared with existing 4D generation methods, PRIO generates dynamics faster and follows user actions more faithfully.

</details>


### [48] [Carry the Fine Print: Budget-Aware Visual Token Compression for Document OCR](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25506)
*Yixuan Huang,ShengFan Zhang,Jiawei Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出Select and Compensate两阶段框架：用自适应分辨率+全局重要性+局部对比选锚点，再用邻近被丢弃内容及相对位置补偿，在不增加token的前提下减半视觉token仍近无损完成整页OCR。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉token压缩把显著性或相似性当作转录重要性的代理，但完整文档OCR中，小而重复的符号虽视觉不显著却对转写不可或缺，该假设失效。

Method: 两阶段解耦“选哪里保留”与“锚点承载多少证据”。选择阶段结合自适应编码分辨率、全局重要性与局部对比度挑选保留token作锚点；补偿阶段用邻近被丢弃内容及其相对位置丰富锚点，不新增token；OCR主干冻结，保留原生坐标与结构token。

Result: 5个主干上评估。半token时Ovis上OmniDocBench Overall 95.42，未压缩96.09；优于单纯降分辨率。原输入网格下75%保留仅差0.17，25%时超学习式全局选择器4.70分。200页固定负载50%预算下仅3个输出触长度上限（RTPrune/DivPrune/HiPrune为30-34），批处理快35-40%。

Conclusion: 选择与补偿可分离，能在激进压缩下保持完整文档转写质量并提升效率，优于分辨率削减与现有剪枝适配方案。

Abstract: Visual token compression often treats salience or similarity as a proxy for transcription importance. Complete document OCR breaks this assumption: small symbols and repeated entries can be indispensable to the transcript despite being visually inconspicuous. We present Select and Compensate, a two-stage framework built on a simple idea: choosing where to retain a token need not determine all the evidence it carries. Selection combines adaptive encoding resolution with global importance and local contrast to choose which tokens to retain as anchors. Compensation enriches these anchors with nearby discarded content and its relative position, without adding tokens. The OCR backbone remains frozen, and native coordinates and structural tokens are preserved. We evaluate complete page transcription across five backbones on OmniDocBench and document reconstruction on MPDocBench. With half the original visual tokens, our input-adapted configuration reaches 95.42 OmniDocBench Overall on Ovis versus 96.09 for the uncompressed model, and paired evaluations show gains over simply reducing the input resolution on both benchmarks. With the original input grid, our method stays within 0.17 points of the uncompressed model at 75% retention and exceeds a learned global selector by 4.70 points at 25%. On a fixed 200-page Ovis workload at the same 50% visual-token budget, only 3 of its outputs hit the length limit, versus 30-34 for the evaluated RTPrune, DivPrune, and HiPrune adaptations, and it finishes the batch 35-40% sooner. Code will be made publicly available.

</details>


### [49] [Persona Lenses: What Makes a Vision-Language Model See Through Its Persona's Eyes?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25508)
*Yoonho Shin*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文提出"人格透镜"(persona lenses)——文本透镜、像素透镜与权重透镜,将人格设定插入模型与网页之间以增强对模拟注视位置的调控,并系统比较三种透镜的效果与模型规模的关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉语言模型可用于模拟用户在网页上的注视位置,但仅在提示中加入人格设定对模拟注视的影响有限("reach"不足),需要研究如何更有效地让人格属性影响模型生成的扫视路径。

Method: 以两个仅差一个属性的MatrAIx人格之间模型注视差异度量人格的"影响范围"(reach);提出三类透镜:文本透镜(两步提示,先让模型写出该人格会看什么)、像素透镜(在与人格兴趣匹配区域上标记)、权重透镜(带门控的注视奖励以保证扫视路径自然)。在生成网页与真实网页上,用Qwen3-VL(4B、32B)与Qwen3.5-9B比较各透镜之间、以及与模型规模(32B无透镜)的差异。

Result: 文本透镜对人格区分度最强,但对不应改变注视的画像变动不鲁棒;像素透镜区分度较弱,但不受与注视无关属性的影响;在4B与9B上,权重透镜能在训练未见的兴趣上区分人格,且在新页面上无需额外步骤;32B模型仅用基础提示即可区分人格。

Conclusion: 透镜的选择取决于成本与模型规模:小模型需借助透镜(如权重透镜)实现人格可控性,大模型可依赖基础提示;不同透镜在区分度与鲁棒性之间存在权衡。

Abstract: Vision-language models have been used to simulate where a user looks on a web page. However, a persona in the prompt reaches the simulated gaze only partly. We measure this reach as the difference in one model's gaze between two MatrAIx personas that differ in a single attribute. We introduce persona lenses, which place the persona between the model and the page: a text lens (a two-step prompt in which the model first writes what the persona would look at), a pixel lens (marks on the region matching the persona's interests) and a weight lens (a dwell reward gated to keep the scanpath natural). Using Qwen3-VL (4B and 32B) and Qwen3.5-9B on generated and live pages, we compare the lenses with one another and with scale, the 32B model with no lens. It has been confirmed that the text lens separates the personas strongly but is not robust to profile changes that should not move gaze. The pixel lens separates the personas less but is unaffected by attributes unrelated to gaze. At 4B and 9B, the weight lens separates the personas on interests held out from training, with no extra step on a new page. The 32B model separates the personas with the base prompt alone. Therefore, the choice of a lens depends on cost and model size.

</details>


### [50] [Mesh-Free Keypoint Learning for Category-Level 6D Object Pose Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25513)
*Junghoon Seo,Hakjin Lee,Jaehoon Sim*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: MORSEPose提出直接从标准位姿与度量尺寸标注构造物体感知重建的支撑集，用归一化物体坐标下的容差膨胀位姿-尺寸包络筛选观测点，替代传统基于表面距离的选择器，在多级RGB-D特征、自适应关键点与可学习类别上下文框架下取得有竞争力结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 类别级6D位姿估计需要将未见实例的局部RGB-D观测关联到类别共享的规范坐标系，物体感知重建有助于自适应关键点保持可见表面几何。但常见重建目标依赖"观测点到已配准实例表面的距离"来选择观测点，需要实例级表面配准，构造不便且对配准质量敏感。

Method: 1) 将观测3D点映射到归一化物体坐标（NOC）；2) 用位姿与度量尺寸标注构造带容差膨胀的位姿-尺寸包络，保留落在包络内的点作为支撑；3) 该构造在相机坐标系与度量尺度间保持一致；4) 证明当标注范围包围物体表面时，包络保守地包含传统表面距离选择器的支撑集；5) 与多级RGB-D特征、自适应关键点和可学习类别上下文集成。

Result: 在REAL275与HouseCat6D上，包络监督在点级与任务级均与基于表面的监督高度匹配；在边界扰动与视角变化下保持稳定；跨基准取得有竞争力的类别级位姿精度。

Conclusion: 基于标准位姿与尺寸标注的包络支撑选择可作为表面距离选择器的实用替代，无需额外实例表面配准即可获得等价的重建监督，并具备尺度一致性与鲁棒性。

Abstract: Category-level 6D pose estimation relates partial RGB-D observations of unseen instances to a canonical frame shared across a category. Object-aware reconstruction helps adaptive keypoints retain the geometry of the visible surface. Common reconstruction targets select observed points by their distance to a registered instance surface. We present MORSEPose, which derives this support directly from standard pose and metric-size annotations. We map observed 3D points to normalized object coordinates and retain those inside a tolerancedilated pose-and-size envelope. This construction is consistent across camera frames and metric scales. When the annotated extent bounds the object surface, the envelope conservatively contains the support of a conventional surface-distance selector. We integrate this target with multi-level RGB-D features, adaptive keypoints, and learnable category context. Experiments on REAL275 and HouseCat6D show that the envelope closely matches surface-based supervision at both point and task levels, remains stable under boundary perturbations and viewpoint changes, and supports competitive category-level pose accuracy across benchmarks.

</details>


### [51] [Multi-View Relighting with Mergeable Radiance Fields](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25523)
*Yinan Wang,Rohan Kadkol,Hyunjin Yoo,Jean-François Lalonde,James J. Clark*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: MergeableRF 通过"参数合并"实现辐射场重光照：为每个基光训练一个外观网络（共享几何、相同初始化），并引入 merge-aware 训练使参数组合对应图像组合，从而用目标环境图的基光系数一次性合并出重光照模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 光照传输具有线性性：混合光下的图像等于各基光图像的加权组合。据此希望直接在辐射场参数层面完成重光照，但独立训练的辐射场参数相加并不产生对应图像组合，故需专门设计使其可合并。

Method: 1) 共享几何 + 每个基光一个外观网络，统一初始化；2) merge-aware 训练，监督外观网络参数的线性组合复原对应图像组合；3) 给定目标 HDR 环境图，计算其基光系数并合并外观网络为单一重光照模型；4) 同时支持 NeRF 与 3DGS。

Result: 在合成 Neural Gaffer 物体与真实 OLATverse 采集上与基于相同多光观测训练的方法重光照质量相当；每视角仅需一次渲染，无需材质估计、额外重光照网络或目标特定优化。

Conclusion: 参数空间合并可作为图像空间混合的高效替代，在保持竞争性重光照质量的同时显著降低推理开销，并具备对 NeRF/3DGS 的通用性。

Abstract: We propose MergeableRF, a method for radiance field relighting through model parameter merging. Given multi-view images of a scene under known basis lights, our goal is to relight the scene by directly combining the parameters of radiance fields trained under these lights. This is motivated by the linearity of light transport: the image under a mixture of lights is the same weighted combination of the images under each light. However, combining the parameters of independently trained radiance fields does not produce the corresponding combination of images. To address this, we represent a scene with shared geometry and a separate appearance network for each basis light. We start these networks from the same initialization and introduce merge-aware training, which teaches their parameter combinations to reproduce the corresponding combinations of images. Given a target HDR environment map, we compute its basis-light coefficients and use them to merge the appearance networks into a single relit model. Our method supports both Neural Radiance Fields (NeRFs) and 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS), without material estimation, additional relighting networks, or target-specific optimization. Unlike image-space blending, it requires only one rendering pass per viewpoint. Experiments on synthetic Neural Gaffer objects and real OLATverse captures demonstrate relighting quality competitive with methods trained on the same multi-light observations.

</details>


### [52] [Contrast Where It Matters: Visual Evidence-Guided Decoding for Mitigating Hallucinations in LVLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25524)
*Yiheng Li,Wenhao Wang,Xingyi Li,Mingda Jia,Zichang Tan,Yinhang Tang,Zhen Lei,Yang Yang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出免训练框架 VEGD：先定位与查询相关的视觉证据，再在对比解码的两个分支中定向引导对该证据的使用，从而一致降低 LVLM 幻觉。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LVLM 幻觉源于语言先验压过视觉证据，或预测依据无关/误导性图像区域。现有免训练对比解码只改动辅助输入或计算方式，既不保证任一分支使用与查询相关的视觉证据，也不能确保对比真正针对该证据。

Method: 1) 证据定位：聚合具有强视觉响应且空间结构一致的定位头以确定查询相关证据区域；2) 在辅助图像中对该区域选择性弱化；3) 生成时将两分支的视觉承载头（visual-carrier heads）引导至同一证据位置——主分支获取完整证据，辅助分支锚定被弱化版本；4) 自适应分布融合：依据分布散度自适应选择融合或对比两分支预测。

Result: 在多种 LVLM 与多类基准上，VEGD 一致减少幻觉，优于现有免训练对比解码方法。

Conclusion: 显式定位并全程引导查询相关视觉证据，可使对比解码的“证据—先验”分离更精准，是缓解 LVLM 幻觉的有效免训练途径。

Abstract: Despite strong multimodal capabilities, large vision-language models (LVLMs) often hallucinate when linguistic priors outweigh visual evidence or when predictions are grounded in irrelevant or misleading image regions. Training-free contrastive decoding mitigates hallucinations by contrasting the original prediction with an auxiliary one that exposes linguistic priors. Yet existing methods focus on altering the auxiliary input or computation and do not ensure that either branch uses query-relevant visual evidence during decoding. We propose Visual Evidence-Guided Decoding (VEGD), a training-free framework that identifies query-relevant visual evidence and guides its use throughout contrastive decoding. VEGD locates this evidence by aggregating localization heads with strong visual responses and coherent spatial structure, then selectively weakens it in the auxiliary image. During generation, VEGD directs visual-carrier heads in both branches toward the same evidence location, strengthening access to intact evidence in the main branch while anchoring the auxiliary branch to its weakened counterpart. An adaptive distribution fusion strategy then combines or contrasts their predictions according to distribution divergence. Experiments across diverse LVLMs and benchmarks demonstrate that VEGD consistently reduces hallucinations.

</details>


### [53] [Support-Guided Triadic Decoding for Hallucination Mitigation in Multimodal Dialogue with LVLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25535)
*Yiheng Li,Wenhao Wang,Xingyi Li,Zhilin Tang,Zichang Tan,Zhen Lei,Yang Yang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出免训练的Support-Guided Triadic Decoding (SGTD)框架，通过在解码时选择性抑制不可靠对话历史并融合多路预测，在多模态多轮对话中兼顾抑制幻觉传播与保留有用上下文。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态多轮对话中，对话历史具有双重作用：可靠回答提供有用上下文，幻觉回答则导致错误跨轮传播。现有免训练方法对历史影响进行全局调整，难以在抑制误导信息的同时不削弱有用上下文，存在明显的权衡问题。

Method: SGTD包含四个组件：(1) Historical Answer Support Estimation，以类型匹配的候选答案为参照，衡量历史回答的视觉支持度并识别可能误导后续生成的低支持内容；(2) Support-Targeted Attention Intervention，削弱该内容的影响同时保留周边对话，得到支持度精炼的预测；(3) Triadic Decoding，将该预测与全历史预测、无答案视觉证据三路融合；(4) Support-Adaptive Plausibility Constraint，利用视觉一致性与历史支持度过滤不合理token。

Result: 在MMHalSnowball与VisDiaHalBench两个基准上，于多类代表性LVLM进行实验，SGTD在含幻觉历史的设定下均取得最高准确率，同时保留事实性上下文带来的收益。

Conclusion: 细粒度、支持度引导的解码控制优于全局历史调整，能够有效缓解幻觉跨轮传播并维持有用上下文的作用，且无需额外训练即可即插即用于现有LVLM。

Abstract: In multimodal multi-turn dialogue, dialogue history plays a dual role: reliable responses provide useful context for subsequent turns, whereas hallucinated responses can propagate errors across turns. Existing training-free methods globally adjust the effect of dialogue history on generation, making it difficult to suppress misleading information without weakening useful context. To address this trade-off, we introduce Support-Guided Triadic Decoding (SGTD), a training-free framework that selectively controls the influence of unreliable history and fuses complementary predictions during decoding. Specifically, Historical Answer Support Estimation measures the visual support of historical responses against type-matched alternatives and identifies low-support content that may mislead later generation. Support-Targeted Attention Intervention then weakens the influence of this content while preserving the surrounding dialogue, producing a support-refined prediction. Triadic Decoding combines this prediction with the full-history prediction and answer-free visual evidence, while the Support-Adaptive Plausibility Constraint filters implausible tokens using visual agreement and historical support. Experiments on MMHalSnowball and VisDiaHalBench across representative LVLMs show that SGTD consistently achieves the highest accuracy under hallucinated histories while preserving the benefits of factual context.

</details>


### [54] [Query-Aware vs. Query-Agnostic Training-Free Video Token Pruning in Multimodal LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25537)
*Divya Kothandaraman,Timo Kumkel,Jaclyn Pytlarz*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该文系统比较了21种免训练的视频token剪枝策略，发现按原始问题嵌入计算的query相关性不可靠，而基于查询分解得到的语义锚点可规避该失败；但query并非必然有利，强query-agnostic覆盖方法在全场比较中仍具竞争力。据此提出实用设计规则：默认保持视觉覆盖，仅在任务与预算需要选择性证据检索时引入结构化相关性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视频大模型需处理大量视觉token，token剪枝对高效推理至关重要。现有免训练剪枝方法在是否及如何使用用户查询上做法不一，query条件化是否真正带来收益尚不明确，缺乏受控对比研究。

Method: 在共享Qwen-3-VL骨干、相同token预算与解码设置下，对21种免训练、LLM前置的视频token选择原则进行受控比较，划分为query-agnostic、浅层query条件化、结构化query条件化与整体化四类。为实例化后两类，提出Structured Query Pruning（将问题分解为语义锚点）及其Pareto变体（融合锚点相关性与query-agnostic视觉多样性）。评测于Video-MME、MVBench、LongVideoBench。

Result: 基于原始问题嵌入的相关性不可靠，比最佳方法低8–16分；基于查询分解的锚点可避免此失败。但query访问并非固有优势，强query-agnostic覆盖方法在全场比较中保持竞争力甚至更优。相比仅冗余基线，结构化选择在紧预算下的证据特定与长上下文任务收益最大；在广泛任务上，整体化选择器优于单一相关性或冗余策略。此外剪枝可降低计算成本与内存。

Conclusion: 提出实用设计规则：默认保持视觉覆盖，当任务类型与token预算需要选择性证据检索时再叠加结构化相关性。为免训练视频token剪枝的策略选型提供了受控证据与可操作准则。

Abstract: Video Large Language Models process large numbers of visual tokens, making token pruning important for efficient inference. Training-free pruning methods differ in whether and how they use the user query, and the value of query conditioning remains unclear. We present a controlled study of training-free, pre-LLM video token selection. Using a shared Qwen-3-VL backbone, token budgets, and decoding settings, we compare 21 pruning principles spanning query-agnostic, shallow query-conditioned, structured query-conditioned, and holistic strategies. To instantiate the last two categories, we introduce Structured Query Pruning, which decomposes the question into semantic anchors, and a Pareto variant, which combines anchor relevance with query-agnostic visual diversity. Across Video-MME, MVBench, and LongVideoBench, relevance computed from the raw question embedding is unreliable (8--16 points below the best methods), whereas anchors derived from query decomposition avoid this failure. However, query access is not inherently advantageous: strong query-agnostic coverage methods remain competitive or superior in the full-field comparison. Relative to a redundancy-only baseline, structured selection helps most on evidence-specific and long-context tasks at tight budgets; on broad tasks, the holistic selector improves over either relevance or redundancy alone. Further, pruning reduces cost and memory. These results support a practical design rule: preserve visual coverage by default, and add structured relevance when the task and token budget demand selective evidence retrieval.

</details>


### [55] [Anchored Inertial Proposals with Objective Verification for Diffusion-Prior Inverse Problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25551)
*Minwoo Kim,Seunghyeok Shin,Dabin Kim,Hongki Lim*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出“锚定惯性校正”规则，将提议生成与接受验证分离，用于免训练扩散先验求解逆问题，在不增加去噪器调用的情况下实现稳定且具竞争力的重建。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 免训练扩散先验在逆问题中很强，但反向采样时的测量引导是局部的：激进更新虽能改善即时数据拟合，却会破坏后续去噪过程。

Method: 在每个校正步，以去噪器预测作为锚点，构建冻结的干净空间目标（测量拟合项 + 噪声水平相关的近端项）；用由测量曲率计算的步长与动量的重球动力学生成干净状态候选；再由独立的验证器以同一目标评估该候选，去噪器预测作为回退；两阶段源自整条轨迹的同一目标并逐步处理；被接受的干净状态重新加噪以继续采样。适用于像素与潜空间扩散，需可微测量算子，无需重训练，且校正循环内不额外调用去噪器。

Result: 理论分析表明目标验证可带来局部非增性与位移控制；在自然图像基准上取得有竞争力的重建质量。

Conclusion: 通过将引导更新的“提议”与“接受”解耦并以统一目标锚定，可在免训练条件下兼顾数据拟合与去噪稳定性，适用于多种扩散范式。

Abstract: Training-free diffusion priors are powerful for inverse problems, but measurement guidance during reverse sampling is local: aggressive updates can improve immediate data fit while disrupting later denoising. We introduce an anchored inertial correction rule that separates proposal generation from acceptance. At each corrected step, the denoiser prediction anchors a frozen clean-space objective combining measurement fit with a noise-level-dependent proximal term. Heavy-ball dynamics with step size and momentum computed from the measured curvature generate a clean-state candidate; a separate verifier evaluates it with the same objective, with the denoiser prediction retained as fallback; both stages are derived from one objective for the whole trajectory, considered one step at a time. The accepted clean state is re-noised to continue sampling. The rule applies to pixel and latent diffusion with differentiable measurement operators, requires no retraining, and adds no denoiser calls inside the correction loop. We analyze how objective verification yields local nonincrease and displacement control. Across natural-image benchmarks, the method achieves competitive reconstruction quality. Code is available at https://github.com/Seunghyeok0715/PIVOT2026.

</details>


### [56] [TwoPass: Draft-Guided Few-Step Video Prediction for Robotic Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25553)
*Yijun Liu,Shijia Ge,Tongtong Feng,Xin Wang,Yuan Meng,Yuwei Liu,Zhi Wang,Wenwu Zhu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: TwoPass：自条件视频预测框架，用自身草稿作条件，4 NFE 达 SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有动作条件视频预测器从噪声去噪时未利用对未来的自身估计，导致预测质量（尤其被操纵物体）受限，且需大量网络调用。

Method: 保持标准 flow-matching 目标的两阶段自条件训练与推理框架：Self-Generated Draft Training 用共享网络在同一噪声状态输出 detached 干净视频估计作条件；Refinement-Only Late Adapter 在有草稿时施加专门残差变换；推理端 Draft-Reuse Iterative Refinement 从每次速度预测同时得到求解器更新与下一草稿；训练采样离散求解器查询噪声水平并更新预训练骨干。

Result: 在 Bridge 与 Language Table 上取得 SOTA，每视频块仅 4 NFE，远少于现有预测器的 50–59 次调用。

Conclusion: 自条件草稿复用可在显著降低采样开销的同时提升动作条件视频预测质量，验证了以自身估计引导去噪的有效性。

Abstract: Action-conditioned video prediction lets robots anticipate the visual consequences of their actions for planning and policy evaluation. Existing predictors denoise each future from noise without using their own estimate of it, which limits the quality of the predicted future, in particular of the manipulated objects, and requires many network calls. We present TwoPass, a self-conditioned training-and-inference framework that keeps the standard flow-matching objective. Self-Generated Draft Training supplies a detached clean-video estimate from the shared network as conditioning at the same noisy state. A Refinement-Only Late Adapter applies a dedicated residual transformation when a draft is present. At inference, Draft-Reuse Iterative Refinement obtains both a solver update and the next draft from each velocity prediction, requiring as few as 4 function evaluations (NFE) per video chunk. Training samples discrete solver-query noise levels and updates the pretrained backbone. Extensive experiments on Bridge and Language Table show that TwoPass achieves state-of-the-art results with 4 NFE, far fewer than the 50-59 calls of existing predictors. Code: https://github.com/PlantPotatoOnMoon/TwoPass.

</details>


### [57] [UniReFP: Robust Unified Fingerprinting for Vision Models against Cross-Task Repurposing Attacks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25555)
*Ziye Geng,Guang Yang,Yihang Chen,Lingfeng Yao,Xing Shi,Miao Pan,Xin Fu,Changqing Luo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出首个跨任务模型重用攻击，并给出统一模型指纹框架 UniReFP 以在异构输出空间下实现所有权验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有模型指纹方法依赖原任务的输出空间，当受保护模型被重用于其他任务（跨任务模型重用攻击）时指纹失效，缺乏跨任务的所有权验证手段。

Method: UniReFP 利用共享的类级语义空间而非任务特定输出空间承载所有权证据：通过重用受保护模型构造代理分类器，使指纹生成脱离原任务输出空间；优化分组指纹，使同组指纹在代理分类器上产生一致的语义响应与中间表示、在独立训练的参考分类器上响应分散；验证阶段采用任务感知语义投影，将异构可疑模型输出映射为统一语义存在向量，并以组内一致性判定所有权。

Result: 实验表明 UniReFP 在跨任务重用攻击、常见模型修改攻击及其组合下均持续取得优于基线的性能。

Conclusion: UniReFP 是适用于多任务视觉模型的统一指纹框架，可有效应对跨任务重用与模型修改攻击，弥补原任务特定指纹的适用性缺陷。

Abstract: We identify, for the first time, a new model modification attack--the cross-task model repurposing attack--that can render fingerprints generated by existing model fingerprinting approaches inapplicable. To address this challenge, we propose UniReFP--a novel unified model fingerprinting framework applicable to diverse vision models across different tasks--that utilizes ownership evidence in a shared class-level semantic space rather than original task-specific output space, enabling effective ownership verification when a protected model is repurposed across tasks with heterogeneous output formats. Specifically, UniReFP constructs a surrogate classifier by repurposing the protected model, enabling fingerprints to be generated independently of the original task-dependent output space of the protected model. Moreover, it optimizes grouped fingerprints to encode ownership evidence: fingerprints within the same group are encouraged to induce consistent semantic responses and intermediate representations on the surrogate classifier, while producing dispersed responses on independently trained reference classifiers. During verification, UniReFP uses a task-aware semantic projection mechanism to map heterogeneous suspect-model outputs into unified semantic-presence vectors, and determines ownership by measuring inner-group consistency. Extensive experimental results validate the effectiveness of UniReFP, consistently showing superior performance under cross-task repurposing attacks, commonly studied model modification attacks, and even their combinations.

</details>


### [58] [Online Mistake Detection in Instruction-Conditioned Procedural Video](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25567)
*Amit Feldman,Ilan Shimshoni,Rotem Dror*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文研究自我中心视频中的在线、指令条件化错误检测，比较模块化流水线与联合模型，联合模型在二分类错误检测上更优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 助手需在操作者仍有时间纠正时指出错误，因此要求边看视频边对照任务指令进行判断：识别所尝试步骤、时间定位并评估执行。现有在线检测器不使用指令文本，而指令条件化方法只能离线运行。

Method: 仅利用已观测视频、任务指令和历史预测，在线识别并定位已完成的动作尝试，分类为正确、修改、失误、纠正或附加；遗漏步骤在视频结束后判定。对比两条路线：模块化流水线（步骤识别、时间定位、执行验证分离）与单步预测所有输出的联合模型；并重新划分 EgoPER 使各数据划分均含错误视频。

Result: 两种方法步骤分割 F1@0.5 相近（93.6% vs 92.1%）；联合模型二分类错误检测 F1@0.5 更高（84.1% vs 80.7%），在 slip 上增益最大（56.4% vs 37.5%）。组分分析表明阶段间误差传播导致模块化方法检测分数偏低。含错误测试视频上，两种设计均优于公开的 AEM 与 ESTANet 检查点。

Conclusion: 联合建模优于模块化流水线，主要因避免了阶段间误差传播；在线指令条件化错误检测可行，但遗漏步骤仍需离线判定。

Abstract: An assistant monitoring a person performing a procedure is most useful when it can detect mistakes while there is still time to recover. This requires judging actions against task instructions as the video unfolds, identifying the attempted step, locating it in time, and assessing its execution. Existing online detectors do not use the instruction text, while instruction-conditioned methods operate offline. In this paper, we study online, instruction-conditioned mistake detection in egocentric videos. Using only observed video, task instructions, and prior predictions, our model identifies and localizes completed action attempts and classifies them as correct, modified, slip, corrections, or additions; omitted steps are determined after the video ends. We compare a modular pipeline, with separate step identification, temporal localization, and execution verification, to a joint model that predicts all outputs in a single step. To support supervised learning from mistakes, we repartition EgoPER to include erroneous videos in all splits. The approaches achieve similar step-segmentation F1@0.5 (93.6% and 92.1%), while the joint model achieves higher binary mistake-detection F1@0.5 (84.1% vs. 80.7%), with its largest gain on slips (56.4% vs. 37.5%). Component-level analysis suggests that error propagation between stages contributes to the modular approach's lower detection score. On test videos containing mistakes, both designs outperform released AEM and ESTANet checkpoints under our evaluation protocol.

</details>


### [59] [VidToC: Rethinking Video Token Compression for Multimodal Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25574)
*Zihan Xiao,Boshen Xu,Pei Fu,Jianzhong Ju,Zhenbo Luo,Jian Luan,Qin Jin*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 VidToC——一个受控评测框架，系统比较具代表性的视频视觉 token 压缩算法、LLM 骨干与时间编码策略，在免训练与微调两种设定、三类互补视频理解任务上给出可比较的实证结论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将 MLLM 从图像扩展到视频时，海量视觉 token 带来显存与延迟瓶颈；虽已有大量视觉 token 压缩方法与更高效的 LLM 架构，但既有方法在训练配置、任务与模型上各不相同，缺乏公平可比性；同时混合 Mamba-Transformer 架构的兴起使"压缩策略能否跨骨干迁移"成为待解问题。

Method: 构建受控评测框架 VidToC，统一训练设置与任务协议，对代表性压缩算法、LLM 骨干与时间编码策略进行对照实验，覆盖免训练与微调两种设置，并在三类互补的视频理解任务上评估；进一步分析压缩插入位置、时间编码方式与骨干选择的影响。

Result: 结果显示压缩行为具有显著的任务依赖性；并刻画了压缩插入位置、时间编码与骨干选择各自的影响效应。

Conclusion: 为跨任务、跨训练设定设计与选择视频 token 压缩策略提供实证性指导，并强调统一评测协议对公平比较的必要性。

Abstract: Extending multimodal large language models (MLLMs) from images to videos faces substantial memory and latency challenges due to the massive number of visual tokens. These challenges have driven the development of numerous visual token compression methods and more efficient LLM architectures. However, existing compression methods are often evaluated with different training setups, tasks, and models, making fair comparison difficult. Meanwhile, the growing use of hybrid Mamba-Transformer architectures raises questions about whether compression strategies transfer across backbones. We present VidToC, a controlled evaluation framework for representative compression algorithms, LLM backbones, and time encoding strategies, covering training-free and fine-tuned settings on three complementary video understanding tasks. Our results reveal task-dependent compression behavior and characterize the effects of compression placement, time encoding, and backbone choice. These findings provide empirical guidance for designing and selecting video token compression strategies across tasks and training settings.

</details>


### [60] [QFT-Based Hybrid PointNet-Quantum Neural Networks for LiDAR Point-Cloud Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25578)
*Anurag Kulkarni,Nouhaila Innan,Muhammad Shafique*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该文提出一种融合PointNet与量子线路的混合架构用于LiDAR点云分类，系统比较五种量子傅里叶变换（QFT）线路配置在理想仿真与噪声模型下的表现，揭示QFT配置同时影响分类性能与噪声鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 混合量子-经典架构可将量子特征变换引入复杂数据处理，QFT提供结构化的特征处理方式，但其线路配置如何影响预测性能、量子资源需求与噪声鲁棒性尚缺乏系统研究。

Method: 构建PointNet-量子神经网络架构：经典特征提取+振幅编码+变分量子线路。系统评估五种傅里叶处理配置：No-QFT、Full-QFT-After、QFT-no-swap、IQFT-After、Trainable-QFT；在KITTI、nuScenes、PandaSet三个数据集、三个随机种子下，考察分类性能、类别级行为、线路复杂度及对IBM FakeManilaV2噪声模型的敏感性。

Result: 理想仿真下，IQFT-After在KITTI取得最高加权F1（0.857±0.033），No-QFT为0.838±0.023；去除QFT末端SWAP门可降低线路复杂度，且仅以固定置换形式影响Pauli-Z测量特征，该置换可由分类头吸收；噪声条件下排序改变，IQFT-After性能退化更明显，No-QFT与Trainable-QFT更鲁棒；逐类分析显示少数类表现存在数据集依赖性差异。

Conclusion: 傅里叶处理配置同时影响分类行为与噪声敏感性，凸显线路结构与面向硬件的评估在三维点云混合量子学习中的重要作用。

Abstract: Hybrid quantum-classical learning architectures provide a promising framework for integrating quantum feature transformations into complex data-processing tasks. Among these transformations, the Quantum Fourier Transform (QFT) offers a structured approach to feature processing, motivating an investigation of how its circuit configuration influences predictive performance, quantum resource requirements, and noise robustness. We present a hybrid PointNet-quantum neural network architecture for LiDAR point-cloud classification, combining classical feature extraction, amplitude encoding, and a variational quantum circuit. Five Fourier-processing configurations are systematically evaluated: No-QFT, Full-QFT-After, QFT-no-swap, IQFT-After, and Trainable-QFT. Experiments on KITTI, nuScenes, and PandaSet across three random seeds assess classification performance, class-level behavior, circuit complexity, and sensitivity to the IBM FakeManilaV2 noise model. Under ideal simulation, IQFT-After achieves the highest weighted F1-score on KITTI (0.857 $\pm$ 0.033), compared with 0.838 $\pm$ 0.023 for No-QFT. Removing the final SWAP gates in the QFT circuit reduces circuit complexity while preserving the Pauli-Z measurement features up to a fixed permutation that can be absorbed by the classification head. Under quantum noise, the circuit ranking changes, with IQFT-After exhibiting greater performance degradation and No-QFT and Trainable-QFT showing stronger robustness. Per-class analysis further identifies dataset-dependent differences in minority-class performance. These results demonstrate that Fourier-processing configuration influences both classification behavior and noise sensitivity, highlighting the role of circuit structure and hardware-aware evaluation in hybrid quantum learning for three-dimensional point-cloud data.

</details>


### [61] [Characterizing Age-Conditioned Affective Reasoning in Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25593)
*Shahriyar Zaman Ridoy,Azmine Toushik Wasi,Lin Gu,Ruogu Fang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 评估17个VLM在年龄提示下对图像效价评分的变化能否预测不同年龄人类的实际评分差异：无年龄提示时模型与人类平均效价高度一致（中位r=0.891），但年龄效应层面几乎无对应关系（r介于-0.030至0.093，上限约0.595），提示人口学个性化评估需在单刺激层面与人类对照。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 年龄提示能改变VLM对图像所诱发情绪的评分，但这种改变是否与不同年龄人群在同一图像上的评分差异相符尚不清楚；若模型的人口学个性化缺乏刺激级人类验证，其实际有效性无法确认。

Method: 在900张OASIS图像上，用青年、中年、老年三种年龄提示评估17个VLM，并以822名被试评分估计每张图像的年龄差异；先比较无年龄提示下的整体效价一致性，再在控制图像愉悦度后检验模型年龄效应与人类年龄效应的相关性，并对照理论上限；进一步考察54张"被判为愉悦的敏感图像"及引入其他图像人类评分（600张留出图像）后的改进。

Result: 无年龄提示时模型与人类平均效价高度吻合（中位Pearson r=0.891）；老年提示下17个模型中16个平均效价低于青年提示。但控制图像愉悦度后，模型与人类年龄效应的相关仅-0.030至0.093，远低于约0.595的估计上限。在54张被判为愉悦的敏感图像上，老年提示使模型效价平均下降0.845分，而老年被试的评分比年轻被试高0.443分，方向相反。提供其他图像的人类评分可改善年龄差异方向的一致性，但刺激级保真度仍有限（r_part=0.105，600张留出图像）。

Conclusion: VLM虽能准确追踪人类平均情绪效价，其年龄条件化的评分变化并不预测真实的年龄差异，甚至在高敏感图像上方向相反；基础模型人口学个性化的评估必须在单个刺激层面与人类评分对照，而不能仅依据平均一致性。

Abstract: An age prompt can change how a vision-language model (VLM) rates the emotion evoked by an image. Does the change predict how people of different ages rate that same image? We evaluate 17 VLMs on 900 OASIS images using young-, middle-, and older-adult prompts and estimate image-specific age differences from ratings by 822 participants. Without an age cue, models closely track average human valence (median Pearson $r=0.891$). Under an older-adult prompt, 16 of 17 models assign lower valence on average than under a young-adult prompt. Yet after adjusting for image pleasantness, correlations between model and human age effects range from $-0.030$ to $0.093$, against an estimated ceiling of $0.595$. The discrepancy is clearest on 54 sensitive images rated as pleasant. Across models, the older prompt reduces valence by $0.845$ points on average, while older participants rate these images $0.443$ points higher than younger participants. Providing human ratings from other images improves agreement on the direction of age differences, although image-specific fidelity remains modest on 600 held-out images ($r_{\mathrm{part}}=0.105$). These results show why evaluations of demographic personalization in foundation models need human comparisons at the level of individual stimuli. Project page available at https://shahriyar-zaman.github.io/age-conditioned-emotion-reasoning/

</details>


### [62] [LoopedWAM: Looped World Action Models for Elastic Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25595)
*Anh Dao,Xinxin Liu,Hang Yu,Jeffrey A Chan Santiago,Yuzhang Shang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: LoopedWAM通过在每个专家内循环复用共享的6-block核心（等效30层计算、仅存12块权重），并以弹性自蒸馏训练单一checkpoint支持多档action深度与采样步数预算；0.58B参数、无机器人预训练即在LIBERO达96.1%、RoboTwin 2.0达91.9%、RoboCasaGR1达52.2%，峰值显存较Fast-WAM降低7.4倍，真机AgileX PiPER成功率71.3%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 世界动作模型（WAM）将生成式视频建模与机器人动作预测耦合，但通常依赖大规模video/action transformer，部署效率低。同时视频与动作专家的推理成本高度不对称：视觉特征每次查询只算一次，动作计算则在每个采样步重复，需针对性设计高效结构。

Method: 提出LoopedWAM：每个专家内以循环方式复用共享的六层核心块，执行30层等效深度而仅存12块参数；针对非对称推理成本，允许视频与动作专家采用不同深度。进一步提出弹性自蒸馏（elastic self-distillation），训练单一弹性checkpoint LoopedWAM-E，覆盖不同action深度与采样步数预算，实现多精度-时延工作点。

Result: LoopedWAM仅0.58B策略参数、无机器人预训练：LIBERO 96.1%、RoboTwin 2.0 91.9%、RoboCasaGR1 52.2%；同等推理测量下峰值GPU显存比Fast-WAM低7.4倍。LoopedWAM-E在H100上达96.0%/27.6ms或95.2%/24.7ms；两步采样+四层action循环仅比LoopedWAM低0.5个点以内。真机AgileX PiPER四任务80次试验成功率71.3%。

Conclusion: 权重共享的深层循环可保持竞争力，而激进的action深度压缩需显式预算感知训练；单一弹性checkpoint即可提供多档精度-时延权衡，验证了紧凑循环WAM在高效机器人部署上的可行性。

Abstract: World action models (WAMs) couple generative video modeling with robotic action prediction but typically require large video and action transformers, limiting deployment efficiency. We present LoopedWAM, a compact WAM that recurrently applies a shared six-block core in each expert, executing 30 transformer layers while storing only 12 blocks per expert. To account for their asymmetric inference costs, the video and action experts can operate at different depths: visual features are computed once per query, whereas action computation repeats at every sampling step. We further propose elastic self-distillation, which trains a single elastic checkpoint, LoopedWAM-E, across action-depth and sampling-step budgets. With only 0.58B policy parameters and no robot pretraining, LoopedWAM achieves 96.1% success on LIBERO, 91.9% on RoboTwin 2.0, and 52.2% on RoboCasaGR1, while using $7.4\times$ less peak GPU memory than Fast-WAM under matched inference measurements. On LIBERO, LoopedWAM-E achieves 96.0% success in 27.6 ms or 95.2% in 24.7 ms on an H100, providing multiple accuracy-latency operating points from a single checkpoint; with two sampling steps and four action loops, it stays within 0.5 points of LoopedWAM on all three benchmarks. Ablations demonstrate that weight-shared depth preserves competitive performance, while aggressive action-depth reduction requires explicit budget-aware training. On a real AgileX PiPER arm, LoopedWAM succeeds in 71.3% of 80 trials across four tasks.

</details>


### [63] [CaT-Movie: Balancing Global Planning and Local Rendering for Long Multi-Shot Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25620)
*Jinpeng Lu,Haoyuan Shi,Yuanshen Guan,Jiyuan Xia,Bin Wu,Yinda Chen,Zhiwei Xiong*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出CaT-Movie，一个"全局故事规划器+局部窗口渲染器"的多镜头长视频生成系统，实现长时间跨度电影的一致性与连贯性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多镜头视频生成器单次仅能渲染有限时长，长电影需由局部片段拼接，导致叙事推进与视觉身份在片段间漂移。

Method: 1) 全局规划器用大语言模型扩展用户请求，保持/推断/补全故事细节，并用确定性代码维护分镜、编译自包含镜头提示、从已生成历史中索引参考帧；2) 局部渲染器采用参考路由稀疏自注意力与镜头级交叉注意力，从索引帧注入身份并隔离各镜头文本条件；3) 构建35万+带语义状态转移、实体定位、多粒度请求标注的电影镜头数据集，并建立CaT-MBench基准（仅在切镜开启新机位时计入跨镜头一致性）。

Result: 在CaT-MBench上，CaT-Movie取得最高平均分，并在所有方法中角色一致性最佳。

Conclusion: 通过规划与渲染解耦、参考帧路由与严格的跨镜头一致性评测，CaT-Movie有效缓解长时程多镜头生成的叙事与身份漂移问题。

Abstract: Current multi-shot video generators render only limited durations per pass, requiring long movies to be stitched from local segments across which narrative progression and visual identity drift. To synthesize long-horizon movies with Coherence across Time, we present CaT-Movie, a multi-shot generation system consisting of a global story planner and a local window renderer. The planner expands user requests via a large language model to preserve, infer, and complete story details, while deterministic code maintains the resulting storyboard across shots to compile self-contained shot prompts and index reference frames from generated history. To execute this guidance, the renderer applies reference-routed sparse self-attention and shot-wise cross-attention to route identities from the indexed frames while isolating per-shot textual conditions. Given the scarcity of high-quality open-source multi-shot data, we construct a dataset of over 350K film shots annotated with semantic state transitions, entity grounding, and multi-granularity requests. We further build CaT-MBench, a benchmark on held-out films with the same annotations. It gives every baseline its native format and counts cross-shot consistency only when a cut opens a new camera setup, preventing repeated framing from passing as consistency. On CaT-MBench, CaT-Movie attains the highest average score and the most consistent characters among all methods. The project page is available at https://jinplu.github.io/CaT-Movie/.

</details>


### [64] [DAWN: Robust Neural Blind Deconvolution via Alternating Wiener Guidance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25621)
*Yida Pan,Yixuan Jia,Chaoyan Huang,Qing Qu,Ismail Alkhouri*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出免训练DIP框架DAWN，通过将基于模型的估计直接注入网络输入并与网络自身先验交替优化，结合对图像/核网络的自编码正则，在高噪声盲图像去卷积中显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高噪声条件下BID仍是病态逆问题，现有方法易噪声过拟合且核估计不准；基于DIP的免训练方法多把模型估计作为额外损失项，未能充分利用其信息。

Method: DAWN在模型输入注入与网络先验精化之间交替；对图像网络和模糊核网络施加自编码正则以得到更可靠的核估计，从而支持该注入机制。

Result: 在自然图像与科学图像、不同强度高斯与泊松噪声下，图像与核质量均优于SOTA；Levin数据集σ=0.1时相比次优基线提升至多+4.5 dB；无训练图像条件下在FFHQ上与大规模分布内训练的扩散模型具有竞争力。

Conclusion: 将模型估计作为输入注入而非仅作损失约束，配合自编码正则，可有效缓解高噪声下的核估计误差与过拟合，为免训练BID提供有效范式。

Abstract: Blind image deconvolution (BID) is an ill-posed inverse problem that aims to jointly recover a sharp image and blur kernel from a single degraded observation. Under nontrivial and high noise, which is common in photography and scientific imaging such as microscopy, BID remains particularly challenging, as existing methods suffer from noise overfitting and inaccurate kernel estimation. Existing training-data-free learning methods for BID, such as those based on the deep image prior (DIP), often utilize external guidance by including model-based estimates as additional loss terms, which underexploits the information these estimates carry especially under nontrivial noise. To address these challenges, we propose DAWN, a training-data-free DIP framework that alternates between injecting model-based estimates directly into the model input and refining this imperfect estimate with the network's own prior. To provide more reliable kernel estimates for these injections, we apply autoencoding regularization to both the image and kernel networks. On natural and scientific images under varying levels of Gaussian and Poisson noise, DAWN outperforms state-of-the-art methods in both image and kernel quality (achieving up to +4.5 dB over the next best baseline on the Levin dataset at $σ= 0.1$). Without any training images, DAWN remains highly competitive with a state-of-the-art diffusion model on FFHQ, despite the latter being trained on a massive number of in-distribution images.

</details>


### [65] [Physical Laws without Physics Loss? Probing Learned Dynamics in Video World Models through Forced Response](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25626)
*Yutao Li,Chen Wang,Wangmeng Zuo,Hui Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文通过"非线性受迫响应"任务检验视频世界模型是否真正学到物理规律：用双模态（视觉-数值）条件微调六个图生视频骨干，仅用流匹配损失训练，并用基于泰勒展开的三级测试诊断其学到的动力学。结果显示所有模型都能复现线性响应（含共振），五个模型能捕捉高阶动力学（含非线性软化），但所学动力学在状态估计或传播出错时可能失效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 仅凭运动与外观指标无法判断视频世界模型是"模仿运动"还是"学到物理规律"，需要一个可分辨二者的受控物理实验范式来检验其物理理解能力。

Method: 构建非线性受迫响应任务：激励使悬挂物体的支点振动；引入视觉-数值双模态条件，将六个图生视频骨干适配到该预测任务，训练仅使用流匹配损失；并基于动力学与几何的泰勒展开设计三级测试（线性响应、高阶动力学、更复杂系统如双摆与摇摆块），辅以反事实测试与消融。

Result: Wan 能准确预测运动，并泛化到未见激励、物体、姿态与场景；反事实测试表明其能力超越对训练视频的复现。三级测试中六个模型均复现线性响应（含共振），五个捕捉高阶动力学（含非线性软化），尽管软化在训练数据中仅占摆动的平方位移的 0.74%。对双摆与摇摆块的扩展表明，持续动力学比短暂冲击被学得更忠实。

Conclusion: 视频世界模型可仅靠流匹配损失学到超出训练分布的动力学结构（线性响应、共振、高阶软化），但所学动力学在状态估计或传播失败时可能不显现；作者据此将测试与发现转化为改进视频训练与生成的策略。

Abstract: Do video world models learn physical laws without a physics loss, or merely imitate motion? Overall motion and appearance scores cannot settle it. We investigate this through nonlinear forced response: an excitation shakes the support of a suspended object. We introduce dual visual-numeric conditioning and adapt six image-to-video backbones to predict this motion using only the flow-matching loss. Wan predicts motion accurately and generalizes to unseen excitations, objects, poses, and scenes. Counterfactual tests suggest capabilities beyond replaying training videos. But what laws have they learned? We design three tiers of tests using Taylor expansions of the dynamics and geometry. All six reproduce the linear response, including resonance; five capture higher-order dynamics, including nonlinear softening. Remarkably, softening is barely present in training: only 0.74% of the swing's squared displacement. Extensions to a double pendulum and a rocking block show sustained dynamics are learned more faithfully than brief impacts. Yet learned dynamics may fail to appear when state estimation or propagation goes wrong. We turn these tests and findings into strategies to improve video training and generation.

</details>


### [66] [RelGeo: Pair-Conditioned Relational Calibration for Frozen Diffusion Correspondence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25628)
*Zixuan Dai,Zhiliang Zhang,Daosong Hu,Tang Zheng,Xinkai Liu,Tianyu Lei,Wei Zhan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 冻结扩散描述子虽能检索强语义对应候选，但余弦最近邻（NN）匹配会在存在有效高分替代时选错位置。作者提出 RelGeo 成对条件化关系校准框架，将扩散互惠亲和与 RoMa 传输等异质关系表达为共享源-目标坐标，其内积在保持冻结描述子全域 NN 搜索的前提下增强相似度。十项评测平均提升 5.51 PCK@0.10 点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 诊断出"候选选择瓶颈"：在 DIFT-SD2.1、DiTF-FLUX 于 SPair-71k、AP-10K、SOCO（CC、SOC、Cross-SOC）上，41–69% 的 Top-1 错误在原始 Top-5 空间峰值中仍保留 PCK@0.10 有效的匹配。且描述子排序与当前成对关系证据（互惠扩散 Query/Key 关联、预训练 RoMa 传输）偏好不同候选，二者具有互补性。

Method: RelGeo：成对条件化关系校准框架。用谱关系坐标（SRC）编码互惠扩散亲和，用形变恒等坐标（WIC）编码可靠性加权的双向传输端点，将异质关系统一为共享源-目标坐标；以二者内积增广冻结描述子相似度，保留全域余弦 NN 匹配，无需额外语义对应训练。

Result: 十项评测全部提升，平均 +5.51 PCK@0.10；固定候选重排序与成对错配对照实验验证了选择质量提升及对当前成对关系对齐的依赖性。

Conclusion: 语义对应瓶颈在于候选选择而非描述子表征；无需重训练即可通过成对关系校准改善冻结扩散描述子的匹配选择。

Abstract: Frozen diffusion descriptors retrieve strong semantic correspondence candidates, yet cosine nearest-neighbor matching can select incorrect locations despite valid high-ranking alternatives. Across DIFT-SD2.1 and DiTF-FLUX on SPair-71k, AP-10K, and SOCO (CC, SOC, Cross-SOC), 41-69% of Top-1 errors retain a PCK@0.10-valid match among the original Top-5 spatial peaks, revealing a persistent candidate-selection bottleneck. We find that descriptor rankings and current-pair relational evidence can favor different candidates: reciprocal diffusion Query/Key associations and pretrained RoMa transport exhibit distinct, partly complementary preferences. To incorporate this evidence into matching decisions, we introduce RelGeo, a Pair-Conditioned Relational Calibration framework expressing heterogeneous relations as shared source-target coordinates. Spectral Relational Coordinates (SRC) encode reciprocal diffusion affinities, while Warp Identity Coordinates (WIC) encode reliability-weighted bidirectional transport endpoints. Their inner products augment frozen descriptor similarity while preserving full-domain cosine nearest-neighbor matching without additional semantic-correspondence training. RelGeo improves all ten evaluations by an average of 5.51 PCK@0.10 points; fixed-candidate reranking and pair-misalignment controls corroborate improved selection and dependence on current-pair relational alignment.

</details>


### [67] [What One Sample Cannot See: Why Fewer Denoising Steps Can Score Higher on Physics Benchmarks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25632)
*Yutao Li,Chen Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 指出Physics-IQ视频世界模型评测指标存在"少步数反而得分更高"的悖论，用精确分解证明其会奖励生成结果多样性的坍缩，并提出Kernel Physics-IQ指标以消除该激励。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视频生成模型作为世界模型评测时出现反常现象：减少去噪步数导致视频模糊或过早静止，物理合理性分数却升高。根源在于Physics-IQ将每个生成视频与单一录像比较，而真实场景存在多个有效未来，指标因而在期望上奖励生成分布方差收缩，即使分布本身更不准确。

Method: 对Physics-IQ得分做精确分解，揭示其含有的方差收缩奖励项；提出Kernel Physics-IQ，基于核方法估计生成分布与真实未来分布的差异，每个条件仅需两个独立采样即可在期望上消除该激励；并在五个图像到视频模型、不同采样步数与采样调度，以及已知结果概率的受控物理系统上进行验证。

Result: 两步采样使Kernel Physics-IQ在全部五个图像到视频模型上下降；CogVideoX官方分数上升0.055而新指标下降0.131；改变采样调度时官方分数忽略的差异被新指标捕捉；在已知结果概率的受控系统中，新指标偏好真实分布而非坍缩预测。

Conclusion: 世界模型的评判不应只看单个视频与已发生结果的吻合度，还应衡量其预测对"可能发生"的分布刻画是否忠实。

Abstract: When a storm can head north or south, a forecast down the middle may score better under squared error than either plausible path. The same paradox appears when video generators are tested as world models: fewer denoising steps can raise physics scores even as videos blur or stop moving early. Why? Physics-IQ compares each generated video with one recording, although a scene can have multiple valid futures. An exact decomposition shows its scores can reward reduced variation among generated futures, even when their distribution becomes less accurate. We introduce Kernel Physics-IQ to remove this incentive in expectation, needing only two independent samples per condition. Two-step sampling lowers Kernel Physics-IQ in all five image-to-video models. For CogVideoX, the official score rises by 0.055 while ours falls by 0.131. Changing the sampling schedule reveals further differences official scores miss. In controlled physical systems with known outcome probabilities, our metric favors the true distribution over collapsed predictions. In short, a world model should be judged not only by how closely one video matches what happened, but by how faithfully its predictions represent what could have happened.

</details>


### [68] [AesDistill: Distilling Generative Aesthetics for Image Color Enhancement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25635)
*Chuanzhi Xu,Chengkun Yue,Huiqi Liang,Haoyang Li,Zihan Deng,Qingwen Zeng,Pengfei Ye,Weidong Cai*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 AesDistill 框架，将大型生成模型的审美能力蒸馏到图像色彩增强任务中，用 4 个开源大生成模型构建 64,644 张编辑图像的教师数据集，并训练 1.13M 参数的学生模型，在 12 项美学与 4 项图像质量评测下超越全部 20 个基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有色彩增强方法以少量专家标注的真值（如 FiveK、PPR10K）为学习目标，仅反映少数专家偏好、审美覆盖有限，匹配这些目标并不能保证在不同口味与场景下获得更优美学质量；而直接使用大生成模型编辑虽能产生生动结果，却会破坏源图的语义结构。

Method: 以 4 个领先开源大生成模型生成 64,644 张编辑图作为教师目标（约耗费 3,360 H100 GPU 小时），构成 AesDistill Dataset；训练 1.13M 参数的学生模型 AesDistill Student，通过置信度加权监督学习教师的色彩变化，同时保持源图语义结构。

Result: 在联合评估中，每个教师目标均高于 FiveK 与 PPR10K 的专家目标均值；在两个数据集上，AesDistill Student 优于全部 20 个基线方法，排名紧随 4 个教师之后。

Conclusion: 从生成模型蒸馏审美知识可使紧凑学生模型超越以往基于专家目标训练的方法，说明以生成式教师替代专家真值是一条有效路径。

Abstract: Image color enhancement aims to improve visual appeal, but previous methods often learn to reproduce ground-truth expert targets. These targets reflect the preferences of a few experts and cover a limited range of aesthetic choices. Matching them does not ensure stronger aesthetic quality across different tastes and scenes. By contrast, large generative models learn rich visual knowledge from large image collections and often produce vivid, appealing image edits. However, direct generation can change the source image's semantic structure along with its colors. In this paper, we introduce AesDistill, a framework for distilling generative aesthetics into image color enhancement. We use 4 leading open-source large generative models to produce 64,644 edited images as teacher targets at a cost of about 3,360 H100 GPU-hours, forming the AesDistill Dataset. AesDistill also includes AesDistill Student, a 1.13M-parameter model that learns teacher color changes through confidence-weighted supervision while preserving the source image's semantic structure. We evaluate AesDistill with 12 aesthetic and 4 image-quality assessment models. Every teacher's targets rank above the FiveK and PPR10K expert target means in the joint assessment. Across both datasets, AesDistill Student outperforms all 20 baseline methods and ranks directly behind the 4 teachers. These results demonstrate that distilling aesthetics from generative models enables a compact student to outperform methods trained on expert targets.

</details>


### [69] [LG-FiLM: Local-Global Mixture-of-Experts Modulation for RAW-to-RGB Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25639)
*Fei Li,Xilei Cao,Shichang Zhai,Kun Zhan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出LG FiLM：在共享编解码器的瓶颈处引入小型仿射专家混合，其缩放、平移与混合权重由局部邻域证据和全局场景描述子预测，使ISP处理模式随场景自适应切换，在Fujifilm UltraISP上取得最优重建保真度并迁移至Zurich RAW to RGB与COCO逆向ISP基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 图像信号处理需按场景自适应决定去噪强度、色彩平衡与色调曲线（如逆光天空与室内人像所需曲线不同）；而多数神经ISP只学习单一固定变换并施加于所有场景，缺乏场景自适应能力。

Method: 将自适应性显式建模进网络：保留单一共享编码器-解码器，在瓶颈处放置小型仿射专家混合（mixture of affine experts）；专家的缩放、平移及混合权重由局部邻域证据与全局场景描述子联合预测；重建始终在共享通路中进行，仅切换处理模式。

Result: 在Fujifilm UltraISP、Zurich RAW to RGB 以及基于逆向ISP构建的 COCO 衍生基准三个数据集上评估；在主基准上取得对比方法中最优的重建保真度，并能迁移到另外两个数据集。

Conclusion: 以轻量瓶颈级仿射专家混合实现场景自适应、共享重建通路的神经ISP设计，可在不牺牲通用性的前提下提升重建保真度并具备跨数据集迁移能力。

Abstract: An image signal processor does more than reconstruct an image. It also has to decide how strongly to denoise, how to balance color, and which tone curve maps the scene onto the display, and these settings depend on the scene itself, the curve that suits a backlit sky is not the one that suits an indoor portrait. Most neural ISPs learn a single fixed transformation and apply it to every scene. We instead make the adaptation an explicit part of the network. Our model, LG FiLM, keeps one shared encoder decoder and places a small mixture of affine experts at its bottleneck; the experts scales, shifts, and mixing weights are predicted from local neighborhood evidence together with a global scene descriptor. Reconstruction stays in a single shared pathway and only the processing mode is switched. We evaluate LG FiLM on three datasets, Fujifilm UltraISP, Zurich RAW to RGB, and a COCO derived benchmark built with an inverse ISP. The model reaches the best reconstruction fidelity of the compared entries on the primary benchmark and transfers to the other two.

</details>


### [70] [TRACE: Attribute Execution for Sequential Image Editing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25648)
*Xiyang Zhang,Yuanhe Tian,Hongzhi Wang,Yan Song*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出TRACE方法，将"持续属性"与"视觉载体"分离，在完整请求校验下优先执行局部外观操作，实现序列式图像编辑中的颜色-材质联合恢复。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 序列图像编辑要求每步既满足新指令又保留仍有效的旧要求，避免用户反复重述或修补。难点在于：检索到的参考图像可能在已过时的表面上带有正确颜色，而当前图像又缺少新请求的材质；仅靠参考选择或精确物体掩码都无法说明同一区域上属性冲突该如何协调。

Method: TRACE包含三个组件：主动目标绑定（active-goal binding）确定哪些属性需持续保留；载体选择（carrier selection）提供合适的当前像素或新生成像素；色调校准（tonal calibration）减弱高光与阴影附近的色偏。整体策略是在完整请求校验下优先执行局部外观操作，分离持续属性与承载它的视觉载体。

Result: 在冻结编辑器、独立视觉读取器、新源图像及完整序列基准上的实验，支持颜色-材质联合恢复，并显示局部执行能够保留周边场景。

Conclusion: 通过属性与载体的解耦及局部优先的执行方式，TRACE在序列编辑中同时实现新请求满足与旧约束保持，且对周围场景扰动小。

Abstract: Given an image and successive user instructions, sequential image editing should return an updated image that satisfies each new request and preserves earlier requirements that remain active. This lets users develop a result without repeatedly restating or repairing their decisions. The difficulty is that a retrieved image can contain a valid color on an obsolete surface, while the current image may lack the newly requested material. Neither reference selection nor a precise object mask alone specifies how to reconcile attributes occupying the same region. We present TRACE, which separates the continuing attribute from its visual carrier and prioritizes local appearance operations under complete-request checks. Active-goal binding identifies what should persist, carrier selection supplies suitable current or newly generated pixels, and tonal calibration reduces tinting near highlights and shadows. Experiments across frozen editors, independent visual readers, new source images, and complete sequential benchmarks support joint color-material recovery and show how local execution preserves the surrounding scene.

</details>


### [71] [SEAF: Selective Semantic Enrichment and Visual Evidence Fusion for Multimodal Sarcasm Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25672)
*Faxian Wan,Xiaocui Yang,Yifan Cao,Shi Feng,Daling Wang,Yifei Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出SEAF双分支框架，用于多图多模态讽刺检测，通过选择性语义增强与视觉证据融合，在MMSD3.0上超越所有对比方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态讽刺检测需要文本、视觉与上下文证据，尤其多图场景下图像提供互补线索。MLLM可生成辅助信息，但其输出混杂有用背景知识与冗余视觉描述，需选择性利用。
