Method: 遵循"一个先验、两种动力学"原则：从同一预训练视频DiT初始化两个模型——逆动力学侧为带可学习动作查询的前馈转移编码器，正动力学侧为因果的动作条件视频生成器；通过控制信息访问（瓶颈）并端到端联合优化两种动力学，形成紧凑动作瓶颈。

Result: 在导航与机器人操作任务上，few-shot动作探测达到SOTA；跨视觉场景与跨具身的零样本动作迁移更强；框架可扩展到V-JEPA2等预测表征先验并取得有竞争力性能。

Conclusion: 复用预训练视频模型（生成式或表征式先验）可同时发现可迁移动作并预测其后果，构成潜动作学习的更广范式。

Abstract: Videos reveal how the world changes, but rarely the actions that cause those changes. Latent action learning recovers such supervision from visual transitions, yet often learns the underlying dynamics largely from scratch. We ask whether a pretrained video generator can instead provide the prior for both inverse and forward dynamics. We introduce O$^{2}$LaF, built on a simple principle: one prior, two dynamics. We initialize both models from the same pretrained video DiT and adapt them to their distinct roles: a feed-forward transition encoder with learnable action queries for inverse dynamics, and a causal, action-conditioned video generator for forward dynamics. By controlling information access and optimizing both dynamics end-to-end through a compact bottleneck, O$^{2}$LaF learns transferable actions from unlabeled videos. Across navigation and robotic manipulation, O$^{2}$LaF sets a new state of the art in few-shot action probing and enables stronger zero-shot action transfer across visual contexts and embodiments. Beyond generative video priors, we further extend our framework to predictive representation priors such as V-JEPA2, achieving competitive performance. Our findings suggest a broader paradigm for latent-action learning: repurposing pretrained video models to discover transferable actions and predict their consequences.

</details>


### [175] [RL Post-Training in Diffusion Transformers Is Activation Steering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26627)
*Tuna Han Salih Meral,Kaan Oktay,Alec Helbling,Atakan Kara,Duen Horng Chau,Pinar Yanardag*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 用一组输入无关的固定 steering 向量（0.29M 参数，比 RL 调过的 LoRA 小 65 倍）即可复现 RL 后训练扩散/视频生成模型 90% 以上的奖励提升，说明 RL 的多数增益本质上是常数偏移而非复杂输入依赖变换。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RL 后训练能显著提升 diffusion transformer 的视觉质量、文字渲染与组合准确性。由于这些能力（按提示词渲染文本、组合多物体）看似依赖输入，通常假设提升它们必须学习复杂的、输入依赖的变换。作者希望量化：仅用一个输入无关的常数偏移能在多大程度上解释这些增益。

Method: 在冻结基模型的每个 block 输出上加入一组固定的 steering 向量（对所有 prompt、token、采样种子完全相同），以匹配公开 RL 调优 checkpoint 的速度场预测为目标进行拟合，目标函数中不含奖励项；另在零初始化下用在线 RL 仅训练这些向量，与基于权重更新的 RL（LoRA）对比。

Result: SD3.5-M 上向量参数量 0.29M，RL-tuned LoRA 为 18.8M（65×）；六个 RL 调优图像模型上复现 90.0–96.3% 的奖励增益，两个视频模型上为 89.8% 与 111.9%；在线 RL 只训练向量时，在 OCR 与 PickScore 上超过权重式 RL 的增益。

Conclusion: 策略梯度式 RL 后训练的大部分收益可由一个常数偏移解释：它无需引入新的输入依赖变换，只是把预训练生成器导向其本已具备的能力。提示对 RL 增益的机制理解与更轻量的对齐方案设计具有意义。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) post-training substantially improves the visual quality, text rendering, and compositional accuracy of diffusion transformers. Because capabilities such as rendering requested text or composing multiple objects are inherently prompt-dependent, a natural hypothesis is that improving them requires learning complex, input-dependent transformations. To isolate how much of these gains an input-independent shift explains, we probe each RL-tuned model with a deliberately simple parameterization: a fixed set of steering vectors added to the block outputs of the frozen base model. On SD3.5-M, these vectors hold 65x fewer parameters than the RL-tuned LoRA (0.29M against 18.8M). We fit them to match the velocity predictions of public RL-tuned checkpoints, with no reward in the objective. Although the vectors are identical for every prompt, token, and sampling seed, they recover 90.0-96.3% of the reward gains of six RL-tuned image models, and 89.8% and 111.9% on two RL-tuned video models. Initialized at zero and trained with online RL, the vectors alone exceed the reward gains of weight-based RL on text rendering (OCR) and human preference (PickScore) on SD3.5-M. A constant shift thus suffices for most of the gain of policy-gradient RL post-training: without new input-dependent transformations, it steers the pretrained generator toward what it can already compute.

</details>


### [176] [Posterior Matching Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26629)
*Hidir Yesiltepe,Julius Berner,Arash Vahdat,Pinar Yanardag*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出 Posterior Matching Distillation (PMD)，一种基于分布的扩散蒸馏方法，通过匹配指数倾斜后验均值来监督学生模型的完整条件后验分布，实现少步且支持任意步数的采样。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于分布的扩散蒸馏多采用 reverse-KL 目标，其 mode-seeking 偏差偏向教师的主导模态而牺牲样本多样性，导致生成多样性不足。

Method: PMD 结合 mode-covering 的后验采样与任意步生成。以固定噪声状态为条件，将学生样本的指数重加权平均匹配到教师后验均值；在高斯加噪下，指数倾斜后验均值可等价为一次平移输入上的教师评估。理论证明在开邻域内匹配倾斜均值可唯一确定完整后验，从而无需辅助学生分数网络即可通过一簇均值约束监督整个条件分布；随机学生模型在训练时间步网格内支持任意步采样预算。

Result: 在 SDXL 与 FLUX.2 [klein] 4B 上，PMD 在两种模型的 4 步方法中取得最优 FID，同时在提示对齐与感知质量指标上保持有竞争力或领先的表现。

Conclusion: PMD 通过后验均值匹配在零总体损失下恢复教师完整后验，缓解 reverse-KL 的多样性损失，为少步扩散蒸馏提供了一种无需辅助网络的分布匹配新范式。

Abstract: Diffusion distillation accelerates image and video generation by compressing a pretrained teacher's iterative sampling process into a few network evaluations. Distribution-based methods achieve this by matching the student's output distribution to the teacher's. This matching is often driven by reverse-KL objectives, whose mode-seeking bias favors dominant teacher modes at the expense of diversity across samples. We introduce Posterior Matching Distillation (PMD), a new member of the distribution-based distillation family that combines mode-covering posterior sampling with any-step generation. At zero population loss, PMD recovers the teacher's full posterior of clean images at each trained noise level. Its stochastic student supports any-step sampling budgets within the trained timestep grid without further training. To learn these posteriors, PMD matches exponentially reweighted averages of student samples conditioned on a fixed noisy state to corresponding teacher posterior means. Under Gaussian corruption, exponentially tilted posterior mean is a single teacher evaluation at a shifted input. We prove that, matching tilted means over an open neighborhood uniquely identifies the full posterior. PMD thus supervises the entire conditional distribution through a family of mean constraints, without any auxiliary student-score network. Experiments on SDXL and FLUX.2 [klein] 4B demonstrate that PMD achieves the best FID among 4-step methods on both models, while maintaining competitive or leading performance across prompt-alignment and perceptual-quality metrics.

</details>


<div id='cs.MM'></div>

# cs.MM [[Back]](#toc)

### [177] [Sonic Palette: Predicting Dynamic Color Themes from Music](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25781)
*Wen-Li Wei,Kenneth Yang,Jen-Chun Lin*

Main category: cs.MM

TL;DR: 提出 Sonic Palette，将音乐到色彩主题的生成建模为跨模态序列预测任务，用音乐条件自回归 Transformer 逐帧预测由五个主色组成的色彩主题，并构建音频—色彩配对数据集与调色板引导的重上色可视化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有音乐—色彩对应研究多将其视为静态映射问题，忽略了视觉色彩属性随音乐随时间演变的过程，因此需要建模时序动态的色彩主题。

Method: 将 music–color 对应表述为跨模态序列预测；使用以音乐为条件的自回归 Transformer，从输入音频特征预测逐帧色彩主题（每帧五个主色）；构建由 265 个完整官方音乐视频衍生的音频—色彩序列配对数据集；并通过调色板引导的重上色流程在保持图像结构的同时可视化时序色彩变化。

Result: 定量结果显示预测色彩主题更接近真值；主观实验表明时序色彩过渡更自然，且音乐—色彩氛围一致性感知更强。

Conclusion: Sonic Palette 验证了时序化、序列化的音乐—色彩建模的有效性，在音乐驱动的视觉设计、计算艺术与创意视觉创作中具有应用潜力。

Abstract: We present Sonic Palette, a music-driven framework for predicting time-varying color themes from audio. Rather than treating music-to-palette generation as a static problem, our method formulates music--color correspondence as a cross-modal sequence prediction task that models how palette-level visual attributes evolve over time in response to music. Specifically, Sonic Palette uses a music-conditioned autoregressive Transformer to predict frame-wise color themes, each represented by five dominant colors, from input audio features. To support this task, we curate a dataset of paired audio--color sequences derived from 265 full-length official music videos. We further visualize the predicted color themes through a palette-guided recoloring process that preserves image structure while revealing temporally varying color appearance associated with the music. Quantitative results show that Sonic Palette predicts color themes that more closely match the ground truth, while subjective results indicate more natural temporal color transitions and stronger perceived music--color atmosphere alignment, highlighting its potential for music-driven visual design, computational art, and creative visual authoring.

</details>


### [178] [PSM-WSM: Predictively Sufficient Multimodal World Social Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25835)
*Chunlei Meng,Zhenyu Yu,Pengbin Feng,Jacqueline J. Pang,Jiabin Luo,Chih-Ting Liao,Zhaolu Kang,Chun Ouyang,Zhongxue Gan*

Main category: cs.MM

TL;DR: 论文提出预测充分性原理，并据此构建PSM-WM框架，联合学习预测性潜状态及其随机时序演化，在多模态交互未来建模上优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态交互系统中，如何学习能支撑未来演化的潜状态仍是开放问题；现有方法多为当前语义理解或直接预测优化表征，未明确定义潜状态应保留何种信息才能保持对未来动态的预测力。

Method: 以"预测充分性"为原则（保留历史多模态观测中与未来相关的信息、降低对观测历史的冗余依赖），提出PSM-WM框架：通过概率状态推断、事件条件转移与多步一致性优化，联合学习预测性潜状态及其随机时序演化；并引入以状态为中心的评估指标——预测闭包、残差历史增益、未来语义对齐。

Result: 在Hi-EF、IEMOCAP、MELD、Ego4D上，PSM-WM提升了未来多模态交互建模效果，所学潜状态具备更强的预测性质与鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 预测充分性是构建面向未来的多模态状态的有效原则，凸显了状态构造在多模态世界建模中的重要性。

Abstract: Learning latent states that support future evolution remains an open problem for multimodal interactive systems. Existing approaches typically optimize representations for current semantic understanding or direct prediction, without explicitly defining what information a latent state should preserve to remain predictive of future dynamics. In this work, we investigate this problem through the principle of predictive sufficiency, which defines a predictive state as a latent variable that retains future-relevant information from historical multimodal observations while reducing redundant dependence on the observed history. Based on this principle, we propose Predictively Sufficient Multimodal Social World Modeling (PSM-WM), a framework that jointly learns predictive latent states and their stochastic temporal evolution through probabilistic state inference, event-conditioned transitions, and multi-step consistency optimization. Beyond forecasting performance, we introduce state-centric evaluations to measure whether learned representations satisfy predictive state properties, including predictive closure, residual history gain, and future semantic alignment. Experiments on Hi-EF, IEMOCAP, MELD, and Ego4D show that PSM-WM improves future multimodal interaction modeling and learns latent states with stronger predictive properties and robustness. These results indicate that predictive sufficiency provides an effective principle for constructing future-oriented multimodal states and highlight the importance of state construction in multimodal world modeling.

</details>
